### [1] [LLM-Driven Large-Scale Spectrum Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13132)
*Ning Yang,Jinliang Gao,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群组相对策略优化（GRPO）的大规模LLM驱动频谱接入（LSA）框架，通过分层状态序列化机制解决大规模场景下的计算崩溃问题，在超密集网络中实现了优于传统方法的频谱效用和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模无线网络中的频谱管理面临着动作空间爆炸和传统优化方法计算不可处理的挑战，现有方法在超大规模场景下存在计算崩溃和冷启动瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出基于群组相对策略优化（GRPO）的大规模LLM驱动频谱接入（LSA）框架，开发分层状态序列化机制，将全局环境统计信息与局部关键约束相融合，使LLM能够在有界的上下文窗口内进行高维推理。

Result: 代码驱动范式消除了SFT冷启动瓶颈，利用直接执行反馈实现优越的扩展定律；框架在不同网络规模下保持稳健的频谱效用和泛化能力，在匹配计算预算下超越非确定性启发式方法，在超密集场景下超越分区经典求解器。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了大规模无线网络频谱管理的计算挑战，证明了LLM驱动方法在频谱接入优化中的有效性，为超密集网络环境下的高效频谱管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient spectrum management in massive-scale wireless networks is increasingly challenged by explosive action spaces and the computational intractability of traditional optimization. This study proposes a Large-Scale LLM-Driven Spectrum Access (LSA) framework rooted in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To overcome the computational collapse caused by ultra-long prompts in large-scale scenarios, we develop a hierarchical state serialization mechanism that synthesizes global environment statistics with localized critical constraints, enabling the LLM to perform high-dimensional reasoning within a bounded context window. Simulation results under strictly time-bounded inference protocols reveal that the code-driven paradigm eliminates the SFT cold-start bottleneck and leverages direct execution feedback to achieve superior scaling laws. The framework maintains robust spectral utility and generalization across varying network scales, yielding consistent and empirically superior performance over non-deterministic heuristics, and surpassing partitioned classical solvers in ultra-dense regimes under matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [2] [The Missing Pillar in Quantum-Safe 6G: Regulation and Global Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13314)
*Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文论证了6G网络的长期运营周期与量子计算威胁的交汇使其面临关键的监管转折点，指出现有静态、增量的合规模式已无法应对长期量子风险，提出必须转向“合规即设计”的系统级范式以实现量子安全的6G网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第六代（6G）移动网络预计将运营数十年，以支持关键任务和全球联邦数字服务。这一长期运营周期恰逢量子计算的快速发展，威胁着现代移动系统的密码学基础。尽管后量子密码学被广泛认为是必要的技术响应，其在6G中的有效部署同样依赖于监管政策和全球合规框架的演进。现有合规模型受静态密码假设、增量演化和时间点认证塑造，难以适应长期量子风险。

Method: 文章基于对电信合规基线挑战的分析、从2G到5G安全监管演进的考察，以及后量子密码学采用的监管影响研究，论证了增量式监管延伸的不足。在此基础上，提出了“合规即设计”视角，将监管要求视为系统级设计约束，强调密码敏捷性、生命周期感知治理、持续合规可观测性以及互操作性驱动的全球保障。

Result: 研究揭示了现有监管框架与量子安全6G网络长期风险之间的结构性错配，论证了增量式监管延伸不足以应对挑战，并提出了以密码敏捷性、生命周期治理、持续合规可观测性和互操作性为核心的设计原则。

Conclusion: 量子安全的6G网络代表着移动网络的监管转折点，必须从传统的静态合规模式转向动态、系统级的“合规即设计”范式。文章警示了全球合规碎片化对量子安全6G网络的风险，强调了在系统设计中内嵌监管约束的重要性。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) mobile networks are expected to operate for multiple decades, supporting mission-critical and globally federated digital services. This long operational horizon coincides with rapid advances in quantum computing that threaten the cryptographic foundations of contemporary mobile systems. While post-quantum cryptography is widely recognized as a necessary technical response, its effective deployment in 6G depends equally on the evolution of regulatory policy and global compliance frameworks. This article argues that quantum-safe 6G represents a regulatory inflection point for mobile networks, as existing compliance models shaped by static cryptographic assumptions, incremental evolution, and point-in-time certification are poorly suited to long-term quantum risk. Building on an analysis of baseline telecom compliance challenges, the evolution of security regulation from 2G to 5G, and the regulatory impact of post-quantum cryptography adoption, the article shows why incremental regulatory extensions are insufficient. To address this gap, the article advances a compliance-by-design perspective in which regulatory requirements are treated as system-level design constraints, emphasizing cryptographic agility, lifecycle-aware governance, continuous compliance observability, and interoperability-driven global assurance, and concludes by examining the risks of fragmented global compliance for quantum-safe 6G networks.

</details>


### [3] [Cross-Domain Query Translation for Network Troubleshooting: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework with Privacy Preservation and Self-Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13353)
*Nguyen Phuc Tran,Brigitte Jaumard,Karthikeyan Premkumar,Salman Memon*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层多智能体LLM架构，通过跨领域查询翻译框架，解决了非技术终端用户与电信领域专家之间的沟通障碍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在私有网络环境中，非技术终端用户与电信领域专家之间存在沟通鸿沟，需要解决用户查询准确分类、隐私保护和专家响应可理解性转换三大挑战。

Method: 采用ReAct风格智能体结合自反思机制进行迭代输出优化，应用满足k-匿名和差分隐私原则的语义保留匿名化技术，并设计了针对有限训练数据场景的少样本学习策略。整个框架基于双阶段分层方法进行用户查询分类。

Result: 该框架在涵盖多个垂直行业的10,000个未见验证场景上进行了全面评估，验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层多智能体LLM架构成功实现了跨领域查询翻译，有效解决了用户隐私保护与诊断实用性之间的平衡问题，为非技术用户与领域专家之间的沟通提供了可行的技术解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical multi-agent LLM architecture to bridge communication gaps between non-technical end users and telecommunications domain experts in private network environments. We propose a cross-domain query translation framework that leverages specialized language models coordinated through multi-agent reflection-based reasoning. The resulting system addresses three critical challenges: (1) accurately classify user queries related to telecommunications network issues using a dual-stage hierarchical approach, (2) preserve user privacy through the anonymization of semantically relevant personally identifiable information (PII) while maintaining diagnostic utility, and (3) translate technical expert responses into user-comprehensible language.
  Our approach employs ReAct-style agents enhanced with self-reflection mechanisms for iterative output refinement, semantic-preserving anonymization techniques respecting $k$-anonymity and differential privacy principles, and few-shot learning strategies designed for limited training data scenarios. The framework was comprehensively evaluated on 10,000 previously unseen validation scenarios across various vertical industries.

</details>


### [4] [Joint Semantic Coding and Routing for Multi-Hop Semantic Transmission in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13361)
*Hong Zeng,Jiangtao Luo,Yongyi Ran*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraphJSCR，一种面向动态低轨卫星网络的图联合路由与语义编码方法，通过图表示学习联合优化下一跳选择、中继处理等级和语义传输预算，实现了语义保真度与传输效率的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星网络中快速变化的拓扑、链路变化和队列动态使得多跳语义传输性能联合依赖于路由、中继处理和语义负载适配，而现有研究通常独立优化路由或语义传输，难以适应局部观测下的动态卫星场景。

Method: 将卫星星座建模为时变有向图，将转发过程建模为部分可观测序贯决策问题；设计图表示学习模块编码局部拓扑、链路状态、队列状况、数据包上下文和语义传输状态；基于学习到的表示，决策网络联合确定下一跳选择、中继处理等级和语义传输预算；参考SwinJSCC框架开发语义编解码器。

Result: 仿真结果表明，GraphJSCR相比基准方法具有更快的收敛速度，在语义保真度和传输效率之间实现了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: GraphJSCR方法有效解决了低轨卫星网络动态环境下多跳语义传输的联合优化问题，为卫星网络中的语义通信提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit satellite networks pose significant challenges to multi-hop semantic transmission because rapidly changing topology, link variability, and queue dynamics make end-to-end performance jointly depend on routing, relay processing, and semantic payload adaptation. Existing studies usually optimize routing or semantic transmission separately and are therefore not well suited to dynamic satellite scenarios under local observations. To address this issue, this paper proposes GraphJSCR, a graph-based joint routing and semantic coding method for multi-hop semantic transmission in dynamic Low Earth Orbit satellite networks. The satellite constellation is modeled as a time-varying directed graph, and the forwarding process is formulated as a partially observable sequential decision problem. A graph representation learning module is designed to encode local topology, link status, queue conditions, packet context, and semantic transmission states. Based on the learned representation, the proposed decision network jointly determines next-hop selection, relay processing level, and semantic transmission budget to balance end-to-end semantic quality and transmission delay. The semantic encoder-decoder is developed with reference to the SwinJSCC framework. Simulation results demonstrate that GraphJSCR achieves faster convergence and a better tradeoff between semantic fidelity and transmission efficiency than benchmark methods.

</details>


### [5] [Agentic Open RAN: A Deterministic and Auditable Framework for Intent-Driven Radio Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13384)
*Hengxu Li,Dongkuan Xu,Mingzhe Chen,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了A1gent，一个面向Open RAN的智能体控制架构，通过将自然语言意图转化为策略并分离非实时推理与近实时执行，实现了可验证、可审计且自治理的无线网络控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）为Open RAN的智能体控制开辟了新途径，使运营商能够以自然语言表达意图，同时将底层执行委托给自主智能体，从而降低操作复杂度并提升网络智能化水平。

Method: 提出A1gent架构，采用推理与实时执行的解耦设计：非实时智能体rApp将运营商目标编译为类型化的A1策略实例；三个任务导向的近实时智能体xApp通过确定性循环执行策略，其中E2接口用于移动性和负载引导，O1接口用于能耗编排。引入编码护栏和固定优先级动作合并器治理冲突，并提出无训练的自适应策略调节器，基于KPI记忆优化有界参数。

Result: A1gent成功实现了意图驱动的规划与确定性近实时执行的集成，在RIC层级间建立了可审计的协调机制，并通过无训练的自适应策略调节器实现了可持续的、可预测的网络参数优化。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合意图驱动规划与确定性近实时执行，推动Open RAN向可验证、自治理和可复现的智能体智能方向迈进，为未来网络自动化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) open new possibilities for agentic control in Open RAN, allowing operators to express intents in natural language while delegating low-level execution to autonomous agents. We present A1gent, an agentic RAN control stack that decouples reasoning from real-time actuation. A non-RT agentic rApp compiles operator goals into typed A1 policy instances, and three task-oriented near-RT agentic xApps enforce them through a deterministic loop with plane-scoped actuation - E2 for mobility and load steering, and O1 for energy orchestration. This agentic reasoning-execution split ensures auditable coordination between RAN intelligent controller (RIC) tiers, supported by encoded guardrails and a fixed-priority action merger for conflict governance. A training-free adaptive policy tuner then refines bounded parameters using KPI memory without retraining, sustaining predictable adaptation. By integrating intent-driven planning with deterministic near-RT execution, A1gent advances Open RAN toward verifiable, self-governing, and reproducible agentic intelligence.

</details>


### [6] [Autoencoder-Based CSI Compression for Beyond Wi-Fi 8 Coordinated Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13500)
*Ibrahim Aboushehada,Boris Bellalta,Giovanni Geraci,Lorenzo Galati Giordano*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自编码器(AE)的CSI压缩机制，用于IEEE 802.11bn标准中的协同波束成形(Co-BF)，可将信道探测开销降低50%以上，显著提升密集Wi-Fi部署中的吞吐量和数据延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集Wi-Fi部署中，协同波束成形(Co-BF)是一种关键的多接入点协调(MAPC)技术，但其性能受到通过重叠基本服务集(OBSS)进行信道探测所需的大量信道状态信息(CSI)反馈的限制。现有的IEEE 802.11 CSI压缩机制由于高信道探测开销，在某些情况下反而不如传统传输方式，这限制了Co-BF的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出了一种基于自编码器(AE)的CSI压缩机制，并将其集成到符合IEEE 802.11bn标准的MAC设计中。使用事件驱动仿真器，结合Sionna RT生成的真实信道，评估了AE重建精度与反馈大小之间的权衡关系，通过测量其对信道探测开销和数据延迟的影响来验证方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，与IEEE 802.11 CSI压缩相比，基于AE的压缩可将信道探测开销降低50%以上。在1/4压缩比下，能够提供最佳的精度与反馈大小权衡，实现最低的数据延迟。与无MAPC的传统传输相比，基于AE的CSI压缩使Co-BF实现了显著的吞吐量增益和数据延迟改善。

Conclusion: 基于自编码器的CSI压缩机制能够有效解决IEEE 802.11 CSI压缩在Co-BF应用中的局限性，通过大幅降低信道探测开销，使Co-BF在吞吐量和延迟方面超越传统传输方式，证明了其作为未来Wi-Fi系统中高效MAPC操作使能者的潜力。

Abstract: Coordinated beamforming (Co-BF) is a key multi-access-point coordination (MAPC) technique for dense Wi-Fi deployments, but its performance can be hindered by the large channel state information (CSI) feedback required through channel sounding across overlapping basic service sets (OBSS). This work proposes an autoencoder (AE)-based CSI compression mechanism integrated into a standards-aligned IEEE 802.11bn MAC design. Using an event-driven simulator with realistic channels generated through Sionna RT, we evaluate the tradeoff between AE reconstruction accuracy and feedback size by measuring their impact on channel sounding overhead and data latency. Our results show that AE-based compression reduces channel sounding overhead by more than 50% compared to IEEE 802.11 CSI compression, with a compression ratio of 1/4 providing the best accuracy/feedback-size tradeoff for lowest data latency. Compared to legacy transmissions without MAPC, IEEE 802.11 CSI compression limits Co-BF due to high channel sounding overhead, causing it to underperform the legacy in some situations. However, AE-based CSI compression enables better Co-BF performance with substantial gains in throughput and data latency compared to legacy, demonstrating its promise as an enabler of efficient MAPC operation in future Wi-Fi systems.

</details>


### [7] [Look One Step Ahead: Forward-Looking Incentive Design with Strategic Privacy for Proactive Service Provisioning over Air-Ground Integrated Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13635)
*Sicheng Wu,Minghui Liwang,Yangyang Gao,Deqing Wang,Wenbo Zhu,Yiguang Hong,Wei Ni,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出LOSA框架，通过前瞻-执行两阶段设计，在保护隐私的前提下实现空地一体化网络中的高效服务匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空地一体化网络中，无人机为地面车辆提供边缘服务面临三大挑战：（1）参与者自利性需要激励机制协调；（2）时空动态变化导致匹配不确定性；（3）现有实时服务依赖精确轨迹信息，引发隐私泄露和决策延迟问题。

Method: 提出前瞻一步（LOSA）框架，将服务提供分解为两阶段：（1）隐私感知前瞻阶段：车辆基于历史效用自适应调整隐私预算，通过轨迹相似度聚类建立一步前瞻协议（OSAA），并构建偏好列表以应对移动不确定性；（2）轻量级实时执行阶段：执行预建立的OSAA和偏好列表解决资源冲突。该框架采用双重拍卖机制，避免重复协商开销。

Result: 理论证明LOSA满足真实性、个体理性和预算平衡特性。在DAIR-V2X、HighD和RCooper等真实数据集上的实验表明，相比基准方法，LOSA在降低交易延迟的同时实现了更优的隐私保护效果。

Conclusion: LOSA框架成功解决了空地一体化网络中服务提供的隐私保护与效率平衡问题，通过前瞻决策与实时执行的解耦设计，在保障机制特性（真实性、个体理性、预算平衡）的前提下，显著降低了计算开销和交易延迟，为动态网络环境下的隐私感知资源分配提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In air-ground integrated networks (AGINs), unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) provide on-demand edge services to ground vehicles. Realizing this vision requires carefully designed incentives to coordinate interactions among self-interested participants. This is exacerbated by the dynamic nature of AGINs, where spatio-temporal variations introduce significant uncertainty in matching UAVs and vehicles. Existing real-time service provisioning typically relies on precise trajectory information, raising privacy concerns and incurring decision latency. To address these challenges, we propose look one-step ahead (LOSA), a novel framework for efficient and privacy-aware service provisioning. By exploiting predictable vehicle travel times between intersections, LOSA decomposes the process into two coupled phases: (i) a privacy-aware look-ahead phase and (ii) a lightweight real-time execution phase. The look-ahead phase allows vehicles to adaptively adjust privacy budgets based on historical utility, balancing trajectory exposure and matching accuracy. Leveraging this, a double auction mechanism establishes binding one-step-ahead agreements (OSAAs) through trajectory similarity clustering, while constructing preference lists to hedge against mobility uncertainty. The execution phase then enforces pre-established OSAAs and preference lists, resolving real-time resource conflicts without costly re-negotiations. This design reduces computational overhead and preserves robustness. We analytically corroborate that LOSA guarantees truthfulness, individual rationality, and budget balance. Experiments on real-world datasets (DAIR-V2X, HighD, and RCooper) demonstrate that LOSA achieves superior privacy protection while lowering transaction latency compared to baseline approaches.

</details>


### [8] [Edge-Side Residual Timing and Frequency Control for Software-Defined Ground Stations in 5G NTN Uplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13984)
*Longji He,Elena Emma Wang,Xichun Wang,Juntao Xu,Jiaming Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了5G非地面网络（NTN）中的地面段实现问题，提出了在地面站边缘闭合残余定时提前量（TA）/载波频率偏移（CFO）闭环控制环的软件定义地面站（SDGS）设计。硬件在环（HIL）实验表明，在深圳稳态跟踪区间内，边缘控制模式将平均RTT从70.51ms降至32.84ms，良好吞吐率从80.14Mbps提升至196.04Mbps，并将残余TA和CFO控制在NR可行范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在5G非地面网络中，低轨卫星（LEO）的高速动态特性给上行链路的定时和频率同步带来挑战。当用户设备（UE）侧的几何预补偿提供了粗略的定时/频率先验后，核心问题是：能否通过边缘侧的残余闭环控制，在快速LEO动态条件下将上行链路维持在NR（新空口）可行的工作区域内？

Method: 采用系统与控制视角的软件定义地面站（SDGS）设计：在UE端保留粗略先验，而在地面站边缘闭合残余TA/CFO闭环控制环。通过2026年3月的硬件在环（HIL）实验活动和配套的不确定性分析来验证方案，实验包含同窗口参考运行作为对照（禁用边缘残余控制），但不包含云端环路基准测试。

Result: 在深圳稳态跟踪区间内，边缘控制模式相比参考配置：平均RTT从70.51±2.34ms降至32.84±2.56ms；在保留的Layer-3传输映射内，良好吞吐率从80.14±0.14Mbps提升至196.04±1.87Mbps。在四个地面站位置上，闭环控制器将残余TA P95保持在0.49μs，残余CFO P95保持在76-77Hz范围内。

Conclusion: 综合HIL实验结果和不确定性分析，支持一个有界声明：在当前HIL实验所保留的假设条件下，边缘侧残余定时/频率闭环能够使SDGS上行链路保持在更稳定的NR可行工作状态。本文的定位声明是架构和控制导向的，而非云端与边缘的直接对比验证。

Abstract: This paper studies a ground-segment implementation problem in 5G non-terrestrial networks (NTN): once UE-side geometric pre-compensation has produced a coarse timing/frequency prior, can an edge-side residual loop keep the uplink inside an NR-feasible operating region under rapid LEO dynamics? We examine this question with a software-defined ground station (SDGS) design that keeps the coarse prior at the UE and closes the residual timing-advance (TA) / carrier-frequency-offset (CFO) loop at the ground-station edge. This paper takes a systems-and-control view rather than proposing a full-stack intelligent architecture. Its evidence base consists of a March 2026 hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) campaign and a companion uncertainty analysis. The HIL campaign includes same-window reference runs collected on the same platform with edge residual control disabled, but it does not include a cloud-loop benchmark. The placement claim is therefore architectural and control-oriented rather than a head-to-head cloud-versus-edge proof. In the Shenzhen steady-state tracking interval, the edge-controlled mode lowers mean RTT from 70.51 +/- 2.34 ms to 32.84 +/- 2.56 ms and, within the retained Layer-3 transport mapping, improves artifact-level goodput from 80.14 +/- 0.14 Mbps to 196.04 +/- 1.87 Mbps relative to that reference configuration. Across four ground-station locations, the closed-loop controller keeps residual TA P95 at 0.49 us and residual CFO P95 within 76-77 Hz. Together with the uncertainty analysis, these observations support a bounded claim: an edge-side residual timing/frequency loop can keep the SDGS uplink in a more stable NR-feasible operating regime under the assumptions retained in the current HIL artifact.

</details>

### [9] [C$^2$T: Captioning-Structure and LLM-Aligned Common-Sense Reward Learning for Traffic--Vehicle Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13098)
*Yuyang Chen,Kaiyan Zhao,Yiming Wang,Ming Yang,Bin Rao,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了C2T框架，利用大型语言模型（LLM）提取“常识”知识并转化为内在奖励函数，用于指导多路口交通信号灯控制的多智能体强化学习（MARL）系统，在交通效率、安全性和能耗方面显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的城市交通控制系统在使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）协调交通信号灯控制器（TLCs）和网联自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）时，性能受到手工设计、短视奖励函数（如路口压力）的根本限制，无法捕捉安全、流稳定性、舒适性等高层次、以人为本的目标。

Method: 提出C2T框架，从交通-车辆动力学中学习常识协调模型。该框架将大型语言模型（LLM）中的“常识”知识蒸馏为学习到的内在奖励函数，以此指导基于CityFlow的多路口MARL系统的协调策略。通过修改LLM提示词，可灵活生成“效率优先”或“安全优先”等不同策略。

Result: C2T框架在基于CityFlow的多路口基准测试中，在交通效率、安全性及能耗相关指标上均显著优于强MARL基线方法。此外，通过调整LLM提示词，框架能灵活实现不同侧重点（如效率导向与安全导向）的策略。

Conclusion: C2T通过引入LLM蒸馏的常识性内在奖励，有效克服了传统MARL方法因手工设计奖励函数带来的局限性，实现了更符合人类高层次目标的交通协调控制，展示了其在多目标策略定制方面的灵活性和优越性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art (SOTA) urban traffic control increasingly employs Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) to coordinate Traffic Light Controllers (TLCs) and Connected Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). However, the performance of these systems is fundamentally capped by their hand-crafted, myopic rewards (e.g., intersection pressure), which fail to capture high-level, human-centric goals like safety, flow stability, and comfort. To overcome this limitation, we introduce C2T, a novel framework that learns a common-sense coordination model from traffic-vehicle dynamics. C2T distills "common-sense" knowledge from a Large Language Model (LLM) into a learned intrinsic reward function. This new reward is then used to guide the coordination policy of a cooperative multi-intersection TLC MARL system on CityFlow-based multi-intersection benchmarks. Our framework significantly outperforms strong MARL baselines in traffic efficiency, safety, and an energy-related proxy. We further highlight C2T's flexibility in principle, allowing distinct "efficiency-focused" versus "safety-focused" policies by modifying the LLM prompt.

</details>


### [10] [Learning Probabilistic Responsibility Allocations for Multi-Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13128)
*Isaac Remy,Caleb Chang,Karen Leung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在多智能体交互中学习概率性责任分配模型的方法，通过条件变分自编码器结合可微分优化层，在无真实责任标签的情况下实现了责任分配的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互环境中，人类行为不仅受个人目标影响，还受与他人共享约束（如安全）的影响。理解人们如何分配责任（即个体为适应他人而偏离期望策略的程度），对于设计社会合规且可信的自主系统具有重要意义。

Method: 利用条件变分自编码器的潜在空间，结合多智能体轨迹预测技术，学习基于场景和智能体上下文的责任分配分布。通过引入可微分优化层，将责任分配映射到可观测的诱导控制，从而在缺乏真实责任标签的情况下保持模型的可处理性。

Result: 在INTERACTION驾驶数据集上的评估表明，该方法不仅实现了优异的预测性能，还通过责任的视角为多智能体交互模式提供了可解释的见解。

Conclusion: 该方法成功捕捉了多智能体交互中固有的多模态不确定性，为理解人类行为中的责任分配提供了新途径，有助于设计更符合社会规范的自主系统。

Abstract: Human behavior in interactive settings is shaped not only by individual objectives but also by shared constraints with others, such as safety. Understanding how people allocate responsibility, i.e., how much one deviates from their desired policy to accommodate others, can inform the design of socially compliant and trustworthy autonomous systems. In this work, we introduce a method for learning a probabilistic responsibility allocation model that captures the multimodal uncertainty inherent in multi-agent interactions. Specifically, our approach leverages the latent space of a conditional variational autoencoder, combined with techniques from multi-agent trajectory forecasting, to learn a distribution over responsibility allocations conditioned on scene and agent context. Although ground-truth responsibility labels are unavailable, the model remains tractable by incorporating a differentiable optimization layer that maps responsibility allocations to induced controls, which are available. We evaluate our method on the INTERACTION driving dataset and demonstrate that it not only achieves strong predictive performance but also provides interpretable insights, through the lens of responsibility, into patterns of multi-agent interaction.

</details>


### [11] [MIND: AI Co-Scientist for Material Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13699)
*Geonhee Ahn,Donghyun Lee,Hayoung Doo,Jonggeol Na,Hyunsoo Cho,Sookyung Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了MIND框架，一个用于材料研究中自动化假设验证的LLM驱动系统，通过集成机器学习原子间势能实现可扩展的计算实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLMs）驱动的科学发现系统主要局限于基于文本的推理，缺乏自动化的实验验证能力。为了克服这一局限性，需要开发能够自动进行实验验证的智能体系统。

Method: 提出MIND框架，将科学发现过程组织为假设细化、实验和基于辩论的验证三个阶段的多智能体流程。系统集成机器学习原子间势能（特别是SevenNet-Omni）实现可扩展的计算实验，并提供基于Web的用户界面。采用模块化设计以便集成额外的实验模块。

Result: 成功实现了材料研究中的自动化假设验证框架，支持可扩展的计算实验验证，并提供了可访问的Web界面和模块化扩展能力。

Conclusion: MIND框架填补了LLM驱动科学发现中实验验证的空白，其模块化设计使其能够适应更广泛的科学工作流程，为自动化科学研究提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic AI systems for scientific discovery, but most approaches remain limited to textbased reasoning without automated experimental verification. We propose MIND, an LLM-driven framework for automated hypothesis validation in materials research. MIND organizes the scientific discovery process into hypothesis refinement, experimentation, and debate-based validation within a multi-agent pipeline. For experimental verification, the system integrates Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials, particularly SevenNet-Omni, enabling scalable in-silico experiments. We also provide a web-based user interface for automated hypothesis testing. The modular design allows additional experimental modules to be integrated, making the framework adaptable to broader scientific workflows. The code is available at: https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MIND, and a demonstration video at: https://youtu.be/lqiFe1OQzN4.

</details>


### [12] [[COMP25] The Automated Negotiating Agents Competition (ANAC) 2025 Challenges and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13914)
*Reyhan Aydoğan,Tim Baarslag,Tamara C. P. Florijn,Katsuhide Fujita,Catholijn M. Jonker,Yasser Mohammad*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第15届国际自动谈判代理竞赛（ANAC 2025）的主要挑战与发现，重点分析了多交易谈判和供应链管理环境下的并发谈判代理。

Method: 组织第15届国际自动谈判代理竞赛（ANAC 2025），聚焦多交易谈判和供应链管理环境下的并发谈判两个关键领域，分析竞赛过程与结果。

Result: 识别了多交易谈判和并发谈判代理开发中的主要研究挑战，分析了竞赛结果，展示了各参赛方案的表现。

Conclusion: 总结了ANAC 2025的关键发现，并为未来竞赛的迭代方向提供了战略性建议，推动自动谈判代理研究的进一步发展。

Abstract: This paper presents the primary research challenges and key findings from the 15th International Automated Negotiating Agents Competition (ANAC 2025), one of the official competitions of IJCAI 2025. We focus on two critical domains: multi-deal negotiations and the development of agents capable of concurrent negotiation within complex supply chain management environments. Furthermore, this work analyzes the results of the competition and outlines strategic directions for future iterations.

</details>

### [13] [Sparse Goodness: How Selective Measurement Transforms Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13081)
*Kamer Ali Yuksel,Hassan Sawaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Forward-Forward算法中goodness函数的设计空间，发现稀疏性是关键设计原则。提出top-k goodness和entmax-weighted energy两种方法，结合FFCL标签注入机制，在Fashion-MNIST上相比基线提升30.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward算法自提出以来一直使用平方和(SoS)作为默认goodness函数，但缺乏对该设计选择的系统性研究。作者希望探索goodness函数的设计空间，寻找比SoS更优的替代方案。

Method: 系统研究goodness函数设计：提出top-k goodness（仅评估最活跃的k个神经元）和entmax-weighted energy（基于α-entmax变换的可学习稀疏加权）；采用FFCL方法通过专用投影在各层注入类别假设；在11种goodness函数、两种架构及稀疏性谱分析上进行对照实验。

Result: top-k goodness相比SoS在Fashion-MNIST上提升22.6个百分点；结合entmax-weighted energy和FFCL，在4x2000架构上达到87.1%准确率，相比SoS基线提升30.7个百分点；发现稀疏性是FF网络goodness函数最重要的设计选择，α≈1.5的自适应稀疏性优于全密集和全稀疏方案。

Conclusion: goodness函数中的稀疏性是FF网络最关键的设计原则，自适应稀疏性（α≈1.5）能够取得最优性能，该发现为FF算法的进一步发展提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm is a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation that trains neural networks layer by layer using a local goodness function to distinguish positive from negative data. Since its introduction, sum-of-squares (SoS) has served as the default goodness function. In this work, we systematically study the design space of goodness functions, investigating both which activations to measure and how to aggregate them. We introduce top-k goodness, which evaluates only the k most active neurons, and show that it substantially outperforms SoS, improving Fashion-MNIST accuracy by 22.6 percentage points. We further introduce entmax-weighted energy, which replaces hard top-k selection with a learnable sparse weighting based on the alpha-entmax transformation, yielding additional gains. Orthogonally, we adopt separate label feature forwarding (FFCL), in which class hypotheses are injected at every layer through a dedicated projection rather than concatenated only at the input. Combining these ideas, we achieve 87.1 percent accuracy on Fashion-MNIST with a 4x2000 architecture, representing a 30.7 percentage point improvement over the SoS baseline while changing only the goodness function and the label pathway. Across controlled experiments covering 11 goodness functions, two architectures, and a sparsity spectrum analysis over both k and alpha, we identify a consistent principle: sparsity in the goodness function is the most important design choice in FF networks. In particular, adaptive sparsity with alpha approximately 1.5 outperforms both fully dense and fully sparse alternatives.

</details>


### [14] [The Long Delay to Arithmetic Generalization: When Learned Representations Outrun Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13082)
*Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transformer中的grokking延迟主要由解码器瓶颈导致：编码器快速习得结构，但解码器访问受阻。通过干预实验和进制选择可显著加速或改善泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解Transformer在算法任务中训练集拟合与突然泛化之间长期延迟（grokking）的根源，该现象虽被观察但其机制尚不清楚。

Method: 在编码器-解码器算术模型上研究单步Collatz预测任务。采用因果干预方法：移植训练好的编码器/解码器到新模型、冻结已收敛编码器单独重训解码器，并跨15种进制对比分析数字表示的影响。

Result: 编码器在数千步内即组织好奇偶和余数结构，但输出准确率在数万步内仍接近随机。移植编码器加速grokking 2.75倍，移植解码器则有害。冻结编码器单独训练解码器消除平台期并达到97.6%准确率。进制选择造成巨大差异：与Collatz算术对齐的进制（如24进制）达99.8%，二进制则完全失败。

Conclusion: Grokking延迟反映的是解码器访问已习得结构的能力受限，而非结构习得本身的失败。数字表示的进制选择作为归纳偏置，控制解码器可利用的局部数字结构程度，从同一底层任务产生巨大的可学习性差异。

Abstract: Grokking in transformers trained on algorithmic tasks is characterized by a long delay between training-set fit and abrupt generalization, but the source of that delay remains poorly understood. In encoder-decoder arithmetic models, we argue that this delay reflects limited access to already learned structure rather than failure to acquire that structure in the first place. We study one-step Collatz prediction and find that the encoder organizes parity and residue structure within the first few thousand training steps, while output accuracy remains near chance for tens of thousands more. Causal interventions support the decoder bottleneck hypothesis. Transplanting a trained encoder into a fresh model accelerates grokking by 2.75 times, while transplanting a trained decoder actively hurts. Freezing a converged encoder and retraining only the decoder eliminates the plateau entirely and yields 97.6% accuracy, compared to 86.1% for joint training. What makes the decoder's job harder or easier depends on numeral representation. Across 15 bases, those whose factorization aligns with the Collatz map's arithmetic (e.g., base 24) reach 99.8% accuracy, while binary fails completely because its representations collapse and never recover. The choice of base acts as an inductive bias that controls how much local digit structure the decoder can exploit, producing large differences in learnability from the same underlying task.

</details>


### [15] [Adaptive Memory Crystallization for Autonomous AI Agent Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13085)
*Rajat Khanda,Mohammad Baqar Sambuddha Chakrabarti,Satyasaran Changdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应记忆结晶（AMC）架构，通过液态-玻璃态-晶体三阶段记忆层级实现持续强化学习中的渐进经验固化，显著提升前向迁移能力，大幅降低灾难性遗忘和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理在动态环境中面临持续挑战：如何在获取新能力的同时不抹除已有知识，即持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 受突触标记与捕获理论启发，将记忆建模为连续结晶过程，提出液态-玻璃态-晶体三阶段记忆层级，通过Itô随机微分方程和Fokker-Planck方程控制经验从可塑态向稳定态迁移。提供理论证明包括：SDE全局收敛至Beta稳态分布、指数收敛速率及Q学习误差界限。

Result: 在Meta-World MT50、Atari 20游戏序列学习和MuJoCo持续运动任务上，相比最强基线：前向迁移提升34-43%，灾难性遗忘降低67-80%，内存占用减少62%。

Conclusion: AMC成功实现了持续强化学习中的渐进经验固化，理论保证与实证效果一致，为解决灾难性遗忘问题提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents operating in dynamic environments face a persistent challenge: acquiring new capabilities without erasing prior knowledge. We present Adaptive Memory Crystallization (AMC), a memory architecture for progressive experience consolidation in continual reinforcement learning.
  AMC is conceptually inspired by the qualitative structure of synaptic tagging and capture (STC) theory, the idea that memories transition through discrete stability phases, but makes no claim to model the underlying molecular or synaptic mechanisms.
  AMC models memory as a continuous crystallization process in which experiences migrate from plastic to stable states according to a multi-objective utility signal. The framework introduces a three-phase memory hierarchy (Liquid--Glass--Crystal) governed by an Itô stochastic differential equation (SDE) whose population-level behavior is captured by an explicit Fokker--Planck equation admitting a closed-form Beta stationary distribution.
  We provide proofs of: (i) well-posedness and global convergence of the crystallization SDE to a unique Beta stationary distribution; (ii) exponential convergence of individual crystallization states to their fixed points, with explicit rates and variance bounds; and (iii) end-to-end Q-learning error bounds and matching memory-capacity lower bounds that link SDE parameters directly to agent performance.
  Empirical evaluation on Meta-World MT50, Atari 20-game sequential learning, and MuJoCo continual locomotion consistently shows improvements in forward transfer (+34--43\% over the strongest baseline), reductions in catastrophic forgetting (67--80\%), and a 62\% decrease in memory footprint.

</details>


### [16] [Design Conditions for Intra-Group Learning of Sequence-Level Rewards: Token Gradient Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13088)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,youwei wang,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏终止奖励下强化学习微调推理模型中长期训练导致的无效更新累积、解概率漂移和熵崩溃问题，提出了组内目标必须保持跨token更新的梯度可交换性这一必要条件，并设计了最小化组内变换来恢复或近似共享token空间中的梯度抵消结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏终止奖励场景下，组内比较已成为通过强化学习微调推理模型的主流范式。然而，长期训练会导致无效更新累积（学习税）、解概率漂移和熵崩溃等问题，现有方法缺乏对token级信用分配的理论指导。

Method: 从token级信用分配视角出发，提出组内目标必须保持跨token更新的梯度可交换性作为算法设计的必要条件，使弱信用/高频token上的梯度能够相互抵消。通过分析破坏可交换性的两种常见机制，提出最小化组内变换来恢复或近似共享token空间中的梯度抵消结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的最小化组内变换能够稳定训练过程、提高样本效率并增强最终性能，验证了梯度可交换性这一设计条件的价值。

Conclusion: 梯度可交换性是设计组内比较算法的必要条件，恢复或近似梯度抵消结构能够有效缓解长期训练中的学习税、概率漂移和熵崩溃问题，为稀疏奖励下的强化学习微调提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: In sparse termination rewards, intra-group comparisons have become the dominant paradigm for fine-tuning reasoning models via reinforcement learning. However, long-term training often leads to issues like ineffective update accumulation (learning tax), solution probability drift, and entropy collapse. This paper presents a necessary condition for algorithm design from a token-level credit assignment perspective: to prevent reward-irrelevant drift, intra-group objectives must maintain gradient exchangeability across token updates, enabling gradient cancellation on weak-credit/high-frequency tokens. We show that two common mechanisms disrupting exchangeability make "non-cancellation" a structural norm. Based on this, we propose minimal intra-group transformations to restore or approximate the cancellation structure in the shared token space. Experimental results demonstrate that these transformations stabilize training, improve sample efficiency, and enhance final performance, validating the value of this design condition.

</details>


### [17] [When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11840)
*Sandro Andric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 增强推理能力的大语言模型在作为多智能体模拟器时可能适得其反：它们会过度优化策略而牺牲行为真实性，而受限反思机制能产生更多样化、更具妥协性的合理行为轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍假设更强的推理能力能提升基于大语言模型的社会经济模拟的逼真度。作者挑战这一假设，指出当模拟目标不是求解最优策略，而是采样符合“有限理性”的合理行为时，推理增强的模型可能成为更好的求解器，却成为更差的模拟器，导致模拟失真。

Method: 研究在三个多智能体谈判环境中进行：模糊分权交易限制场景、模糊统一反对交易限制场景、以及紧急电力管理中的电网限电新域案例。比较三种反思条件（无反思、受限反思、原生推理），横跨两个主要模型系列，并扩展至OpenAI的GPT-4.1和GPT-5.2进行直接测试。

Result: 在所有三个实验中，受限反思产生的轨迹比无反思或原生推理更丰富多样且更具妥协倾向。在OpenAI扩展实验中，GPT-5.2原生推理在三个实验的45次运行中全部以权威决策告终，而GPT-5.2受限反思在每个环境中均恢复了妥协结果。

Conclusion: 模型能力与模拟逼真度是不同目标。在进行行为模拟时，应将模型限定为“采样器”而非“求解器”，否则推理增强可能导致模拟失真。这为基于LLM的行为模拟研究提供了方法论警示。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agents in social, economic, and policy simulations. A common assumption is that stronger reasoning should improve simulation fidelity. We argue that this assumption can fail when the objective is not to solve a strategic problem, but to sample plausible boundedly rational behavior. In such settings, reasoning-enhanced models can become better solvers and worse simulators: they can over-optimize for strategically dominant actions, collapse compromise-oriented terminal behavior, and sometimes exhibit a diversity-without-fidelity pattern in which local variation survives without outcome-level fidelity. We study this solver-sampler mismatch in three multi-agent negotiation environments adapted from earlier simulation work: an ambiguous fragmented-authority trading-limits scenario, an ambiguous unified-opposition trading-limits scenario, and a new-domain grid-curtailment case in emergency electricity management. We compare three reflection conditions, no reflection, bounded reflection, and native reasoning, across two primary model families and then extend the same protocol to direct OpenAI runs with GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.2. Across all three experiments, bounded reflection produces substantially more diverse and compromise-oriented trajectories than either no reflection or native reasoning. In the direct OpenAI extension, GPT-5.2 native ends in authority decisions in 45 of 45 runs across the three experiments, while GPT-5.2 bounded recovers compromise outcomes in every environment. The contribution is not a claim that reasoning is generally harmful. It is a methodological warning: model capability and simulation fidelity are different objectives, and behavioral simulation should qualify models as samplers, not only as solvers.

</details>


### [18] [Spectral Entropy Collapse as an Empirical Signature of Delayed Generalisation in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13123)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Truong Quynh Hoa,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现归一化谱熵可作为预测深度学习“顿悟”现象的标量序参量，在熵值跨越阈值约0.61后可高精度预测泛化发生的时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “顿悟”现象——即在记忆数据很久之后才出现延迟泛化——目前缺乏可预测的机制性解释，本研究旨在寻找并验证一个能刻画和预测该相变的序参量。

Method: 作者提出用表示协方差矩阵的归一化谱熵作为标量序参量，在群论任务上训练单层Transformer，并通过大量实验和因果干预（阻止熵坍缩、规范匹配对照）验证其有效性，同时对比MLP架构以检验机制必要性。

Result: 顿悟分为两个阶段：范数扩张后熵坍缩；熵值在泛化前稳定跨越阈值约0.61（100%实验、平均提前1,020步）；因果干预阻止坍缩会使顿悟延迟+5,020步；幂律公式可在4.1%误差内预测顿悟时机；该机制在阿贝尔与非阿贝尔群任务上均成立；MLP可出现熵坍缩却无顿悟，说明坍缩必要但不充分。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵是刻画顿悟相变的有效序参量，熵坍缩是其必要条件但非充分条件，架构选择对最终泛化至关重要。

Abstract: Grokking -- delayed generalisation long after memorisation -- lacks a predictive mechanistic explanation. We identify the normalised spectral entropy $\tilde{H}(t)$ of the representation covariance as a scalar order parameter for this transition, validated on 1-layer Transformers on group-theoretic tasks. Five contributions: (i) Grokking follows a two-phase pattern: norm expansion then entropy collapse. (ii) $\tilde{H}$ crosses a stable threshold $\tilde{H}^* \approx 0.61$ before generalisation in 100% of runs (mean lead: 1,020 steps). (iii) A causal intervention preventing collapse delays grokking by +5,020 steps ($p=0.044$); a norm-matched control ($n=30$, $p=5\times10^{-5}$) confirms entropy -- not norm -- drives the transition. (iv) A power-law $ΔT = C_1(\tilde{H}-\tilde{H}^*)^γ+C_2$ ($R^2=0.543$) predicts grokking onset with 4.1% error. (v) The mechanism holds across abelian ($\mathbb{Z}/97\mathbb{Z}$) and non-abelian ($S_5$) groups. Crucially, MLPs show entropy collapse without grokking, proving collapse is necessary but not sufficient -- architecture matters. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/grokking-entropy

</details>


### [19] [BioTrain: Sub-MB, Sub-50mW On-Device Fine-Tuning for Edge-AI on Biosignals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13359)
*Run Wang,Victor J. B. Jung,Philip Wiese,Sebastian Frey,Giusy Spacone,Francesco Conti,Alessio Burrello,Luca Benin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BioTrain框架，在毫瓦级功耗和亚兆字节内存约束下实现生物信号模型的全网络微调，在GAP9 MCU平台上相比传统方法将内存占用降低8.1倍，并在新受试者校准中准确率提升高达35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信号存在显著的跨受试者和跨会话变异性，导致严重的域偏移，降低边缘AI模型的部署后性能。现有低功耗MCU平台由于全反向传播的高内存和计算成本，只能支持浅层或稀疏适应方案，无法满足设备端适应需求。

Method: 提出BioTrain框架，通过高效的内存分配器和网络拓扑优化，实现毫瓦级功耗和亚兆字节内存约束下的全网络微调；在GAP9 MCU平台上实现完全片上反向传播，支持大批量训练。

Result: 在EEG和EOG数据集上验证，全网络微调相比非适应基准准确率提升高达35%，新受试者校准时比最后层更新方法准确率约高7%；在GAP9上EEG和EOG模型训练吞吐量分别达17样本/秒和85样本/秒，功耗低于50 mW；内存占用从5.4 MB降至0.67 MB，降低8.1倍。

Conclusion: BioTrain成功在资源极度受限的可穿戴设备上实现了高效的全网络设备端训练，为解决生物信号的域偏移问题提供了实用方案，同时保护用户隐私并确保系统可靠性。

Abstract: Biosignals exhibit substantial cross-subject and cross-session variability, inducing severe domain shifts that degrade post-deployment performance for small, edge-oriented AI models. On-device adaptation is therefore essential to both preserve user privacy and ensure system reliability. However, existing sub-100 mW MCU-based wearable platforms can only support shallow or sparse adaptation schemes due to the prohibitive memory footprint and computational cost of full backpropagation (BP). In this paper, we propose BioTrain, a framework enabling full-network fine-tuning of state-of-the-art biosignal models under milliwatt-scale power and sub-megabyte memory constraints. We validate BioTrain using both offline and on-device benchmarks on EEG and EOG datasets, covering Day-1 new-subject calibration and longitudinal adaptation to signal drift. Experimental results show that full-network fine-tuning achieves accuracy improvements of up to 35% over non-adapted baselines and outperforms last-layer updates by approximately 7% during new-subject calibration. On the GAP9 MCU platform, BioTrain enables efficient on-device training throughput of 17 samples/s for EEG and 85 samples/s for EOG models within a power envelope below 50 mW. In addition, BioTrain's efficient memory allocator and network topology optimization enable the use of a large batch size, reducing peak memory usage. For fully on-chip BP on GAP9, BioTrain reduces the memory footprint by 8.1x, from 5.4 MB to 0.67 MB, compared to conventional full-network fine-tuning using batch normalization with batch size 8.

</details>


### [20] [Synthetic Tabular Generators Fail to Preserve Behavioral Fraud Patterns: A Benchmark on Temporal, Velocity, and Multi-Account Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13125)
*Bhavana Sajja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出行为保真度（behavioral fidelity）作为合成表格数据的第三评估维度，旨在评估生成数据是否保留了真实实体活动的时间、序列和结构行为模式，并证明现有主流行独立生成器在结构上无法复现欺诈检测中的关键行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成表格数据评估框架主要关注统计保真度（边缘分布和相关性）和下游实用性（分类器AUROC），但两者均未测试操作检测和分析系统实际依赖的行为信号。在欺诈检测等场景中，实体活动的时序、序列和结构行为模式至关重要，缺乏对此类模式的评估导致生成数据的实际应用价值受限。

Method: 1. 正式定义四种行为欺诈模式（P1-P4），覆盖事件间隔时间（IET）、爆发结构、多账户图模体和速度规则触发率；
2. 提出退化比率指标，以真实数据噪声地板为基准进行校准（1.0表示匹配真实变异性，k表示劣化k倍）；
3. 从理论上证明行独立生成器（当前主流范式）在结构上无法复现P3图模体（命题1），且产生的实体内IET自相关非正（命题2），导致欺诈序列的正爆发指纹无法实现；
4. 在IEEE-CIS欺诈检测数据集和Amazon欺诈数据集上对CTGAN、TVAE、GaussianCopula和TabularARGN进行基准测试。

Result: 四种生成器均表现严重失败：在IEEE-CIS上，复合退化比率从24.4x（TVAE）到39.0x（GaussianCopula）不等；在Amazon FDB上，行独立生成器得分为81.6-99.7x，TabularARGN为17.2x。研究记录了各生成器特有的失败模式及其解决方案。理论分析表明，无论架构或训练数据规模如何，行独立生成器都无法产生真实的正爆发指纹。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了合成表格数据评估的重要空白，证明了现有生成器在行为保真度方面的根本局限性。P1-P4评估框架可扩展至任何具有实体级序列表格数据的领域（如医疗保健和网络安全）。研究开源了评估框架，为未来改进生成模型的行为保真度提供了理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: We introduce behavioral fidelity -- a third evaluation dimension for synthetic tabular data that measures whether generated data preserves the temporal, sequential, and structural behavioral patterns that distinguish real-world entity activity. Existing frameworks evaluate statistical fidelity (marginal distributions and correlations) and downstream utility (classifier AUROC on synthetic-trained models), but neither tests for the behavioral signals that operational detection and analysis systems actually rely on. We formalize a taxonomy of four behavioral fraud patterns (P1-P4) covering inter-event timing, burst structure, multi-account graph motifs, and velocity-rule trigger rates; define a degradation ratio metric calibrated to a real-data noise floor (1.0 = matches real variability, k = k-times worse); and prove that row-independent generators -- the dominant paradigm -- are structurally incapable of reproducing P3 graph motifs (Proposition 1) and produce non-positive within-entity IET autocorrelation (Proposition 2), making the positive burst fingerprint of fraud sequences unachievable regardless of architecture or training data size. We benchmark CTGAN, TVAE, GaussianCopula, and TabularARGN on IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection and the Amazon Fraud Dataset. All four fail severely: on IEEE-CIS composite degradation ratios range from 24.4x (TVAE) to 39.0x (GaussianCopula); on Amazon FDB, row-independent generators score 81.6-99.7x, while TabularARGN achieves 17.2x. We document generator-specific failure modes and their resolutions. The P1-P4 framework extends to any domain with entity-level sequential tabular data, including healthcare and network security. We release our evaluation framework as open source.

</details>


### [21] [Bridging MARL to SARL: An Order-Independent Multi-Agent Transformer via Latent Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13472)
*Zijian Zhao,Jing Gao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出共识多智能体Transformer（CMAT），通过将协作MARL转化为层次化单智能体RL问题，利用Transformer编码器处理联合观测空间，并通过自回归生成高层共识向量来分解联合动作空间，在多个基准任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作多智能体强化学习（MARL）通过分解集中控制问题来处理大规模联合观测和动作空间，但这种分解引入了非平稳性、训练不稳定、协调性弱和理论保证有限等挑战。现有的多智能体Transformer（MAT）对动作生成顺序敏感，限制了方法的性能和稳定性。

Method: CMAT采用集中式框架，将所有智能体视为统一实体。使用Transformer编码器处理大规模联合观测空间。引入层次化决策机制：Transformer解码器自回归生成高层共识向量，模拟智能体在潜在空间达成策略一致的过程；基于该共识，所有智能体同时生成动作，实现与顺序无关的联合决策。这种分解使得联合策略可以使用单智能体PPO进行优化，同时通过潜在共识保持表达能力强的协调机制。

Result: 在StarCraft II、Multi-Agent MuJoCo和Google Research Football基准任务上的实验表明，CMAT的性能优于最新的集中式解决方案、顺序MARL方法和传统MARL基线方法。

Conclusion: CMAT成功地将协作MARL桥接到层次化单智能体RL框架，通过共识机制实现了与顺序无关的联合决策，在保持强协调能力的同时提供了更好的理论保证，为解决大规模联合空间下的多智能体协作问题提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is widely used to address large joint observation and action spaces by decomposing a centralized control problem into multiple interacting agents. However, such decomposition often introduces additional challenges, including non-stationarity, unstable training, weak coordination, and limited theoretical guarantees. In this paper, we propose the Consensus Multi-Agent Transformer (CMAT), a centralized framework that bridges cooperative MARL to a hierarchical single-agent reinforcement learning (SARL) formulation. CMAT treats all agents as a unified entity and employs a Transformer encoder to process the large joint observation space. To handle the extensive joint action space, we introduce a hierarchical decision-making mechanism in which a Transformer decoder autoregressively generates a high-level consensus vector, simulating the process by which agents reach agreement on their strategies in latent space. Conditioned on this consensus, all agents generate their actions simultaneously, enabling order-independent joint decision making and avoiding the sensitivity to action-generation order in conventional Multi-Agent Transformers (MAT). This factorization allows the joint policy to be optimized using single-agent PPO while preserving expressive coordination through the latent consensus. To evaluate the proposed method, we conduct experiments on benchmark tasks from StarCraft II, Multi-Agent MuJoCo, and Google Research Football. The results show that CMAT achieves superior performance over recent centralized solutions, sequential MARL methods, and conventional MARL baselines. The code for this paper is available at:https://github.com/RS2002/CMAT .

</details>


### [22] [Generalization Guarantees on Data-Driven Tuning of Gradient Descent with Langevin Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13130)
*Saumya Goyal,Rohith Rongali,Ritabrata Ray,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Langevin梯度下降算法（LGD）用于回归问题的元学习，证明了其能够达到贝叶斯最优解，并提供了维度为O(dh)的泛化误差界，将先前弹性网络的结果推广到了一般凸损失回归问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从超参数优化的视角研究回归问题的元学习，旨在为凸回归任务设计一种能够近似后验分布均值并达到贝叶斯最优解的算法，同时提供理论泛化保证。

Method: 提出Langevin梯度下降算法(LGD)，该算法通过损失函数和正则化项定义后验分布并近似其均值；理论上证明了存在最优超参数配置使LGD达到平方损失的贝叶斯最优解；通过伪维度分析提供元学习超参数的泛化保证。

Result: 证明了LGD算法存在最优超参数配置可达贝叶斯最优解；在温和假设下得到伪维度界为O(dh)（对数项除外），该界与先前弹性网络（仅允许h=2个超参数）的结果在维度依赖上匹配，并将界推广到凸损失回归；实验验证了LGD和元学习过程在少样本线性回归上的有效性。

Conclusion: LGD算法为回归问题的元学习提供了理论扎实的方法，其伪维度界O(dh)相比弹性网络实现了从固定2个超参数到任意h个超参数的推广，同时保持相同的维度依赖性，为数据驱动设置下元学习超参数提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study learning to learn for regression problems through the lens of hyperparameter tuning. We propose the Langevin Gradient Descent Algorithm (LGD), which approximates the mean of the posterior distribution defined by the loss function and regularizer of a convex regression task. We prove the existence of an optimal hyperparameter configuration for which the LGD algorithm achieves the Bayes' optimal solution for squared loss. Subsequently, we study generalization guarantees on meta-learning optimal hyperparameters for the LGD algorithm from a given set of tasks in the data-driven setting. For a number of parameters $d$ and hyperparameter dimension $h$, we show a pseudo-dimension bound of $O(dh)$, upto logarithmic terms under mild assumptions on LGD. This matches the dimensional dependence of the bounds obtained in prior work for the elastic net, which only allows for $h=2$ hyperparameters, and extends their bounds to regression on convex loss. Finally, we show empirical evidence of the success of LGD and the meta-learning procedure for few-shot learning on linear regression using a few synthetically created datasets.

</details>


### [23] [Chain of Uncertain Rewards with Large Language Models for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13504)
*Shentong Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CoUR框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）和代码不确定性量化优化强化学习中的奖励函数设计，显著降低了评估成本并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习中的奖励函数设计过程存在效率低、一致性差、依赖大量人工设计且易忽视中间决策点局部不确定性等问题，亟需一种更高效、自动化的方法来简化奖励函数的设计与评估流程。

Method: 提出Chain of Uncertain Rewards (CoUR)框架，集成大语言模型（LLM）以简化奖励函数设计。引入代码不确定性量化与相似性选择机制（结合文本和语义分析），识别并复用最相关的奖励函数组件。通过减少冗余评估，并对解耦的奖励项进行贝叶斯优化，实现更高效的搜索。

Result: 在IsaacGym的9个原始环境及Bidexterous Manipulation基准的全部20个任务上进行了全面评估。实验结果表明，CoUR不仅取得了更好的性能表现，还显著降低了奖励函数评估的成本。

Conclusion: CoUR框架有效地解决了传统奖励函数设计中的冗余与不确定性问题，证明了利用LLM与不确定性量化技术能够显著提升强化学习奖励设计的效率与效果，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions is a cornerstone of reinforcement learning (RL), yet it remains a challenging and labor-intensive process due to the inefficiencies and inconsistencies inherent in traditional methods. Existing methods often rely on extensive manual design and evaluation steps, which are prone to redundancy and overlook local uncertainties at intermediate decision points. To address these challenges, we propose the Chain of Uncertain Rewards (CoUR), a novel framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) to streamline reward function design and evaluation in RL environments. Specifically, our CoUR introduces code uncertainty quantification with a similarity selection mechanism that combines textual and semantic analyses to identify and reuse the most relevant reward function components. By reducing redundant evaluations and leveraging Bayesian optimization on decoupled reward terms, CoUR enables a more efficient and robust search for optimal reward feedback. We comprehensively evaluate CoUR across nine original environments from IsaacGym and all 20 tasks from the Bidexterous Manipulation benchmark. The experimental results demonstrate that CoUR not only achieves better performance but also significantly lowers the cost of reward evaluations.

</details>


### [24] [Depth-Resolved Coral Reef Thermal Fields from Satellite SST and Sparse In-Situ Loggers Using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13131)
*Alzayat Saleh,Mostafa Rahimi Azghadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息神经网络(PINN)，融合卫星海表温度与稀疏原位温度测数据，实现珊瑚礁不同深度的温度精确估算，改善热应力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星海表温度(SST)产品是全球珊瑚白化监测的基础，但仅测量海洋表层温度。珊瑚栖息深度从浅水到20米以下，该深度温度比表层低1-3°C，将卫星SST统一应用于所有深度会高估次表层热应力。

Method: 开发物理信息神经网络(PINN)，将NOAA珊瑚礁观测SST与稀疏原位温度记录器融合到一维垂直热方程中，强制SST作为硬表面边界条件，联合学习有效热扩散率(κ)和光衰减系数(Kd)。在四个大堡礁站点进行30次留出实验验证。

Result: PINN在未见过的深度实现0.25-1.38°C RMSE。在极端稀疏条件下(三个训练深度)，PINN在5米和9.1米留出深度分别保持0.27°C和0.32°C RMSE，而统计基线崩溃至>1.8°C；在90%实验中优于纯物理有限差分基线。深度分辨的度加热日(DHD)剖面显示热应力随深度衰减：Davies礁DHD从表层0.29降至10.7米处的零值，与记录器观测一致，而卫星DHD在所有深度保持0.31不变。

Conclusion: 物理约束的卫星SST与稀疏记录器融合可将白化评估扩展到深度维度，利用现有观测基础设施实现深度分辨的热应力评估。PINN在浅层深度会低估绝对DHD，其预测值应解释为深度分辨应力的保守下限。

Abstract: Satellite sea surface temperature (SST) products underpin global coral bleaching monitoring, yet they measure only the ocean skin. Corals inhabit depths from the shallows to beyond 20 metres, where temperatures can be 1-3°C cooler than the surface; applying satellite SST uniformly to all depths therefore overestimates subsurface thermal stress. We present a physics-informed neural network (PINN) that fuses NOAA Coral Reef Watch SST with sparse in-situ temperature loggers within the one-dimensional vertical heat equation, enforcing SST as a hard surface boundary condition and jointly learning effective thermal diffusivity (\k{appa}) and light attenuation (Kd). Validated across four Great Barrier Reef sites (30 holdout experiments), the PINN achieves 0.25-1.38°C RMSE at unseen depths. Under extreme sparsity (three training depths), the PINN maintains 0.27°C RMSE at the 5 metre holdout and 0.32°C at the 9.1 metre holdout, where statistical baselines collapse to >1.8°C; it outperforms a physics-only finite-difference baseline in 90% of experiments. Depth-resolved Degree Heating Day (DHD) profiles show that thermal stress attenuates with depth: at Davies Reef, DHD drops from 0.29 at the surface to zero by 10.7 metres, consistent with logger observations, while satellite DHD remains constant at 0.31 across all depths. However, the PINN underestimates absolute DHD at shallow depths because its smooth predictions attenuate the short-duration peaks that drive threshold exceedances; PINN DHD values should be interpreted as conservative lower bounds on depth-resolved stress. These results demonstrate that physics-constrained fusion of satellite SST with sparse loggers can extend bleaching assessment to the depth dimension using existing observational infrastructure.

</details>

*Wenqing Li,Xu Feng,Peixue Jiang,Yinhai Zhu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力循环设计方法依赖专家知识或穷举搜索，效率低且缺乏可扩展性，限制了高性能循环的发现。需要开发一种自动化、可扩展的智能设计方法。

Method: 采用图表示法编码热力循环，结合深度学习热物性代理模型，构建分层强化学习框架。高层管理者探索结构演化并提出候选配置，低层工作者优化参数并提供性能奖励，引导搜索向高性能区域收敛。

Result: 以热泵和热机循环为案例，该方法不仅复现了经典循环配置，还分别发现了18种新型热泵循环和21种新型热机循环。与传统循环相比，新型配置的性能分别提升4.6%和133.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效平衡了效率与广泛适用性，为专家驱动的热力循环设计提供了一个实用、可扩展的智能替代方案，实现了从编码、解码到协同优化的全自动流程。

Abstract: Thermodynamic cycles are pivotal in determining the efficacy of energy conversion systems. Traditional design methodologies, which rely on expert knowledge or exhaustive enumeration, are inefficient and lack scalability, thereby constraining the discovery of high-performance cycles. In this study, we introduce a graph-based hierarchical reinforcement learning approach for the co-design of structure parameters in thermodynamic cycles. These cycles are encoded as graphs, with components and connections depicted as nodes and edges, adhering to grammatical constraints. A deep learning-based thermophysical surrogate facilitates stable graph decoding and the simultaneous resolution of global parameters. Building on this foundation, we develop a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework wherein a high-level manager explores structural evolution and proposes candidate configurations, whereas a low-level worker optimizes parameters and provides performance rewards to steer the search towards high-performance regions. By integrating graph representation, thermophysical surrogate, and manager-worker learning, this method establishes a fully automated pipeline for encoding, decoding, and co-optimization. Using heat pump and heat engine cycles as case studies, the results demonstrate that the proposed method not only replicates classical cycle configurations but also identifies 18 and 21 novel heat pump and heat engine cycles, respectively. Relative to classical cycles, the novel configurations exhibit performance improvements of 4.6% and 133.3%, respectively, surpassing the traditional designs. This method effectively balances efficiency with broad applicability, providing a practical and scalable intelligent alternative to expert-driven thermodynamic cycle design.

</details>


### [26] [Pareto-Optimal Offline Reinforcement Learning via Smooth Tchebysheff Scalarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13175)
*Aadyot Bhatnagar,Peter Mørch Groth,Ali Madani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STOMP算法，通过平滑Tchebysheff标量化方法解决多目标对齐问题，在蛋白质工程任务上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的多目标对齐方法主要依赖线性奖励标量化，但该方法在理论上无法恢复帕累托前沿的非凸区域。许多实际应用（如蛋白质工程中同时优化催化活性和特异性，或聊天机器人中同时优化有用性和无害性）需要同时优化多个冲突目标，因此亟需更有效的多目标对齐方法。

Method: 将多目标强化学习本身构建为一个优化问题，采用平滑Tchebysheff标量化技术替代直接线性标量化。基于此提出STOMP算法，通过基于观测分布标准化各个奖励，将直接偏好优化（DPO）原则性地扩展到多目标场景。

Result: 在蛋白质工程任务上，对三个自回归蛋白质语言模型在三个蛋白质适应性实验室数据集上进行验证。STOMP在九个设置中的八个取得了最高的超体积指标，涵盖离线离策略评估和生成式评估两种方式，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: STOMP是一种强大且鲁棒的多目标对齐算法，能够有效改进多属性蛋白质优化等任务中的后训练模型性能，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language models can be aligned with human preferences through offline reinforcement learning (RL) on small labeled datasets. While single-objective alignment is well-studied, many real-world applications demand the simultaneous optimization of multiple conflicting rewards, e.g. optimizing both catalytic activity and specificity in protein engineering, or helpfulness and harmlessness for chatbots. Prior work has largely relied on linear reward scalarization, but this approach provably fails to recover non-convex regions of the Pareto front. In this paper, instead of scalarizing the rewards directly, we frame multi-objective RL itself as an optimization problem to be scalarized via smooth Tchebysheff scalarization, a recent technique that overcomes the shortcomings of linear scalarization. We use this formulation to derive Smooth Tchebysheff Optimization of Multi-Objective Preferences (STOMP), a novel offline RL algorithm that extends direct preference optimization to the multi-objective setting in a principled way by standardizing the individual rewards based on their observed distributions. We empirically validate STOMP on a range of protein engineering tasks by aligning three autoregressive protein language models on three laboratory datasets of protein fitness. Compared to state-of-the-art baselines, STOMP achieves the highest hypervolumes in eight of nine settings according to both offline off-policy and generative evaluations. We thus demonstrate that STOMP is a powerful, robust multi-objective alignment algorithm that can meaningfully improve post-trained models for multi-attribute protein optimization and beyond.

</details>


### [27] [KV Packet: Recomputation-Free Context-Independent KV Caching for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13226)
*Chuangtao Chen,Grace Li Zhang,Xunzhao Yin,Cheng Zhuo,Bing Li,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KV Packet，一种无需重计算的KV缓存复用框架，通过轻量级可训练软令牌适配器将缓存文档封装为不可变“数据包”，在实现接近零FLOPs和更低TTFT延迟的同时，保持了与全重计算基线相当的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）严重依赖键值（KV）缓存来最小化推理延迟，但标准KV缓存具有上下文依赖性：在新上下文中复用缓存文档时需要重新计算KV状态以适应注意力分布的变化。现有解决方案（如CacheBlend、EPIC和SAM-KV）通过选择性重计算部分令牌来缓解此问题，但仍会产生不可忽视的计算开销（FLOPs）并增加首个令牌时间（TTFT）延迟。

Method: 提出KV Packet框架，一种免重计算的缓存复用方法。该方法将缓存文档视为不可变的“数据包”，并用轻量级可训练软令牌适配器进行封装。这些适配器通过自监督蒸馏训练，用于弥合上下文不连续性，从而避免传统方法中的选择性重计算过程。

Result: 在Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5模型上的实验表明，KV Packet方法实现了接近零FLOPs的计算开销，相比基于重计算的基线方法具有更低的TTFT延迟，同时保持了与全重计算基线相当的F1分数。

Conclusion: KV Packet成功解决了标准KV缓存的上下文依赖问题，在无需重计算的情况下实现了高效的缓存复用，显著降低了计算开销和推理延迟，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely heavily on Key-Value (KV) caching to minimize inference latency. However, standard KV caches are context-dependent: reusing a cached document in a new context requires recomputing KV states to account for shifts in attention distribution. Existing solutions such as CacheBlend, EPIC, and SAM-KV mitigate this issue by selectively recomputing a subset of tokens; however, they still incur non-negligible computational overhead (FLOPs) and increased Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) latency. In this paper, we propose KV Packet, a recomputation-free cache reuse framework that treats cached documents as immutable ``packets'' wrapped in light-weight trainable soft-token adapters, which are trained via self-supervised distillation to bridge context discontinuities. Experiments on Llama-3.1 and Qwen2.5 demonstrate that the proposed KV Packet method achieves near-zero FLOPs and lower TTFT than recomputation-based baselines, while retaining F1 scores comparable to those of the full recomputation baseline.

</details>


### [28] [Does Dimensionality Reduction via Random Projections Preserve Landscape Features?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13230)
*Iván Olarte Rodríguez,Anja Jankovic,Thomas Bäck,Elena Raponi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了探索性景观分析（ELA）特征在高维优化问题中通过随机高斯嵌入（RGE）进行降维后的稳健性，发现大多数ELA特征对降维投影高度敏感，即使部分特征看似稳健，也可能反映的是投影引入的伪影而非原始问题的内在特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索性景观分析（ELA）在高维设置中面临稀疏性、估计方差大和计算成本高昂的问题。虽然降维被认为是解决这些问题的途径，但目前尚不清楚在降维空间中计算的特征是否仍然反映原始景观的内在属性。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 研究者利用随机高斯嵌入（RGE）对搜索空间进行降维。在保持采样点和目标函数值不变的前提下，他们在投影空间中计算ELA特征，并将这些特征与在原始搜索空间中获得的特征进行比较。实验涵盖了多种采样预算和嵌入维度。

Result: 结果表明，线性随机投影通常会改变与ELA相关的几何和拓扑结构，导致特征值不再能代表原始问题。虽然有一小部分特征相对稳定，但大多数特征对嵌入高度敏感。更重要的是，投影下的稳健性并不一定意味着该特征具有信息量，看似稳健的特征可能仍然反映的是投影引入的伪影而非景观的内在特征。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，简单的线性随机投影在ELA特征计算中的适用性有限，因为它会显著改变景观结构。未来的研究需要开发更复杂的降维技术或对ELA特征进行改进，以确保其稳健性和信息保留能力。

Abstract: Exploratory Landscape Analysis (ELA) provides numerical features for characterizing black-box optimization problems. In high-dimensional settings, however, ELA suffers from sparsity effects, high estimator variance, and the prohibitive cost of computing several feature classes. Dimensionality reduction has therefore been proposed as a way to make ELA applicable in such settings, but it remains unclear whether features computed in reduced spaces still reflect intrinsic properties of the original landscape.
  In this work, we investigate the robustness of ELA features under dimensionality reduction via Random Gaussian Embeddings (RGEs). Starting from the same sampled points and objective values, we compute ELA features in projected spaces and compare them to those obtained in the original search space across multiple sample budgets and embedding dimensions.
  Our results show that linear random projections often alter the geometric and topological structure relevant to ELA, yielding feature values that are no longer representative of the original problem. While a small subset of features remains comparatively stable, most are highly sensitive to the embedding. Moreover, robustness under projection does not necessarily imply informativeness, as apparently robust features may still reflect projection-induced artifacts rather than intrinsic landscape characteristics.

</details>


### [29] [Analog Optical Inference on Million-Record Mortgage Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13251)
*Sofia Berloff,Pavel Koptev,Konstantin Malkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在大规模真实数据集上基准测试模拟光计算机（AOC），发现精度损失主要源于编码方式和架构设计，而非硬件非理想性，为未来优化指明了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟光计算机在机器学习推理中具有显著的能效优势，但现有研究仅停留在小规模图像基准测试上。为了验证AOC在实际大规模应用中的可行性，需要在一个大规模、真实世界的分类任务上进行严格的性能评估与误差源分析。

Method: 作者在包含584万条美国HMDA记录的抵押贷款审批分类任务上，对AOC数字孪生模型进行基准测试，并系统性地分离了三种精度损失来源：编码限制（127位二进制编码）、架构限制（光学通道宽度）和硬件非理想性（七种校准的硬件缺陷）。通过对比AOC与XGBoost等数字模型在相同条件下的表现，量化各因素的独立影响。

Result: AOC在原始19个特征上达到94.6%的平衡准确率（5,126参数，其中1,024个光学参数），相比XGBoost的97.9%存在3.3个百分点的差距。将光学核心从16通道扩展到48通道仅缩小0.5pp的差距，表明瓶颈在于架构而非硬件。统一使用127位二进制编码后，所有模型准确率降至89.4-89.6%，其中数字模型损失8pp，AOC损失5pp。七种硬件非理想性未造成可测量的精度损失。

Conclusion: 研究明确了模拟光计算机精度损失的三个层次：编码限制影响最大，架构限制次之，硬件保真度影响最小。这为未来研究提供了清晰的优化路径：应优先改进编码方案和架构设计，而非过度关注硬件非理想性的消除。

Abstract: Analog optical computers promise large efficiency gains for machine learning inference, yet no demonstration has moved beyond small-scale image benchmarks. We benchmark the analog optical computer (AOC) digital twin on mortgage approval classification from 5.84 million U.S. HMDA records and separate three sources of accuracy loss. On the original 19 features, the AOC reaches 94.6% balanced accuracy with 5,126 parameters (1,024 optical), compared with 97.9% for XGBoost; the 3.3 percentage-point gap narrows by only 0.5pp when the optical core is widened from 16 to 48 channels, suggesting an architectural rather than hardware limitation. Restricting all models to a shared 127-bit binary encoding drops every model to 89.4--89.6%, with an encoding cost of 8pp for digital models and 5pp for the AOC. Seven calibrated hardware non-idealities impose no measurable penalty. The three resulting layers of limitation (encoding, architecture, hardware fidelity) locate where accuracy is lost and what to improve next.

</details>


### [30] [Out of Context: Reliability in Multimodal Anomaly Detection Requires Contextual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13252)
*Kevin Wilkinghoff,Neelu Madan,Juan Miguel Valverde,Kamal Nasrollahi,Radu Tudor Ionescu,Rafal Wisniewski,Thomas B. Moeslund,Wenwu Wang,Zheng-Hua Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出传统异常检测假设单一参考分布的局限性，提出将多模态异常检测重构为跨模态上下文推理问题，通过区分上下文信息与异常信号实现条件化异常评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常检测方法假设正常行为可由单一无条件参考分布捕获，但在动态异构环境中，异常往往具有上下文依赖性——同一观测在不同运行条件下可能正常或异常。现有方法未能区分上下文信息与异常相关信号，导致结构模糊性问题：无法在边缘建模下区分上下文变化与真实异常，造成性能不稳定和异常评估不可靠。

Method: 提出将多模态异常检测重构为跨模态上下文推理问题，其中模态扮演不对称角色：将上下文信息与观测分离，基于运行条件定义条件化异常，而非相对于单一全局参考进行评估。

Result: 揭示了现有方法在处理上下文依赖性异常时的根本缺陷，明确了多模态数据中模态角色不对称性的重要性，为条件化异常检测提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 该视角对模型设计、评估协议和基准构建具有重要意义，为构建鲁棒的上下文感知多模态异常检测系统指明了开放研究挑战和未来方向。

Abstract: Anomaly detection aims to identify observations that deviate from expected behavior. Because anomalous events are inherently sparse, most frameworks are trained exclusively on normal data to learn a single reference model of normality. This implicitly assumes that normal behavior can be captured by a single, unconditional reference distribution. In practice, however, anomalies are often context-dependent: A specific observation may be normal under one operating condition, yet anomalous under another. As machine learning systems are deployed in dynamic and heterogeneous environments, these fixed-context assumptions introduce structural ambiguity, i.e., the inability to distinguish contextual variation from genuine abnormality under marginal modeling, leading to unstable performance and unreliable anomaly assessments. While modern sensing systems frequently collect multimodal data capturing complementary aspects of both system behavior and operating conditions, existing methods treat all data streams equally, without distinguishing contextual information from anomaly-relevant signals. As a result, abnormality is often evaluated without explicitly conditioning on operating conditions. We argue that multimodal anomaly detection should be reframed as a cross-modal contextual inference problem, in which modalities play asymmetric roles, separating context from observation, to define abnormality conditionally rather than relative to a single global reference. This perspective has implications for model design, evaluation protocols, and benchmark construction, and outline open research challenges toward robust, context-aware multimodal anomaly detection.

</details>


### [31] [Bias-Corrected Adaptive Conformal Inference for Multi-Horizon Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13253)
*Ankit Lade,Sai Krishna J.,Indar Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了偏差校正自适应保形推断（BC-ACI）方法，通过在线估计预测偏差来解决ACI在分布漂移下对称放宽区间的问题，在保持平稳数据性能的同时显著降低了分布漂移场景下的区间评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ACI方法仅能调整分位数阈值而无法移动区间中心，当基准预测器在机制变化后出现持续偏差时，ACI通过对称放宽区间来补偿，导致预测区间过于保守。需要一种方法能够直接纠正偏差而非仅仅处理其症状。

Method: 提出偏差校正ACI（BC-ACI）：在标准ACI基础上增加在线指数加权移动平均（EWM）估计器来估计预测偏差；在分位数计算前校正非一致性分数并重新定位预测区间中心；引入自适应死区阈值，当估计偏差与噪声无法区分时抑制校正，确保在已校准数据上不发生性能退化。

Result: 在688次受控实验中（涵盖两种基准模型、四种合成机制和三个真实数据集），BC-ACI在均值和复合分布漂移下Winkler区间评分降低13-17%（Wilcoxon p < 0.001），在平稳数据上保持等效性能（比率1.002x）。有限样本分析表明覆盖率保证随偏差估计误差优雅退化。

Conclusion: BC-ACI通过在线偏差估计有效解决了分布漂移下的预测区间校准问题，在保持理论覆盖率保证的同时显著提升了预测效率，为时间序列预测中的不确定性量化提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) provides distribution-free prediction intervals with asymptotic coverage guarantees for time series under distribution shift. However, ACI only adapts the quantile threshold -- it cannot shift the interval center. When a base forecaster develops persistent bias after a regime change, ACI compensates by widening intervals symmetrically, producing unnecessarily conservative bands. We propose Bias-Corrected ACI (BC-ACI), which augments standard ACI with an online exponentially weighted moving average (EWM) estimate of forecast bias. BC-ACI corrects nonconformity scores before quantile computation and re-centers prediction intervals, addressing the root cause of miscalibration rather than its symptom. An adaptive dead-zone threshold suppresses corrections when estimated bias is indistinguishable from noise, ensuring no degradation on well-calibrated data. In controlled experiments across 688 runs spanning two base models, four synthetic regimes, and three real datasets, BC-ACI reduces Winkler interval scores by 13--17% under mean and compound distribution shifts (Wilcoxon p < 0.001) while maintaining equivalent performance on stationary data (ratio 1.002x). We provide finite-sample analysis showing that coverage guarantees degrade gracefully with bias estimation error.

</details>
