However, the approach never quite reaches the estimated theoretical optimal performance, and when tested on a real-life domain mismatch between two sleep studies, the benefit was insignificant.
  Significance: 'Discriminator-guided fine tuning' is an interesting approach to handling signal degradation for 'in the wild' sleep monitoring, with some promise. In particular, what it says about sleep data in general is interesting. However, more development will be necessary before using it 'in production'.

</details>


### [106] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Methane Sorption: Cross-Gas Transfer Learning, Ensemble Collapse Under Physics Constraints, and Monte Carlo Dropout Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13992)
*Mohammad Nooraiepour,Zezhang Song,Wei Li,Sarah Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个物理信息迁移学习框架，通过氢气吸附模型迁移预测甲烷吸附，在114个煤实验数据上达到R²=0.932，相比传统方法提升227%，并发现Monte Carlo Dropout是该框架下最佳的不确定性量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨非均质煤阶的甲烷吸附预测需要模型同时具备热力学一致性、稀缺地质系统间的高效知识迁移以及校准的不确定性估计能力，而现有框架很少同时解决这些需求。

Method: 开发了物理信息迁移学习框架，通过弹性权重巩固将氢气吸附PINN适应于甲烷吸附预测，结合煤特定特征工程和三阶段课程学习，在993个平衡测量数据上进行训练，并采用五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法进行比较分析。

Result: 在留出煤样本上达到R²=0.932，相比仅压力的经典等温线模型提升227%；氢气预训练比随机初始化降低18.9%的RMSE和19.4%的收敛时间。Monte Carlo Dropout实现良好校准的不确定性且开销最小，而深度集成因共享物理约束导致性能下降。SHAP和ALE分析证实学习表征物理可解释且与煤吸附机制一致。

Conclusion: Monte Carlo Dropout是物理约束迁移学习框架中表现最佳的不确定性量化方法，跨气体迁移学习为地质材料建模提供了数据高效的策略。

Abstract: Accurate methane sorption prediction across heterogeneous coal ranks requires models that combine thermodynamic consistency, efficient knowledge transfer across data-scarce geological systems, and calibrated uncertainty estimates, capabilities that are rarely addressed together in existing frameworks. We present a physics-informed transfer learning framework that adapts a hydrogen sorption PINN to methane sorption prediction via Elastic Weight Consolidation, coal-specific feature engineering, and a three-phase curriculum that progressively balances transfer preservation with thermodynamic fine-tuning. Trained on 993 equilibrium measurements from 114 independent coal experiments spanning lignite to anthracite, the framework achieves R2 = 0.932 on held-out coal samples, a 227% improvement over pressure-only classical isotherms, while hydrogen pre-training delivers 18.9% lower RMSE and 19.4% faster convergence than random initialization. Five Bayesian uncertainty quantification approaches reveal a systematic divergence in performance across physics-constrained architectures. Monte Carlo Dropout achieves well-calibrated uncertainty at minimal overhead, while deep ensembles, regardless of architectural diversity or initialization strategy, exhibit performance degradation because shared physics constraints narrow the admissible solution manifold. SHAP and ALE analyses confirm that learned representations remain physically interpretable and aligned with established coal sorption mechanisms: moisture-volatile interactions are most influential, pressure-temperature coupling captures thermodynamic co-dependence, and features exhibit non-monotonic effects. These results identify Monte Carlo Dropout as the best-performing UQ method in this physics-constrained transfer learning framework, and demonstrate cross-gas transfer learning as a data-efficient strategy for geological material modeling.

</details>


### [107] [Parameter Importance is Not Static: Evolving Parameter Isolation for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14010)
*Zekai Lin,Chao Xue,Di Liang,Xingsheng Han,Peiyang Liu,Xianjie Wu,Lei Jiang,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi,Shuang Liang,Minlong Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI)框架，通过在线估计参数重要性动态调整隔离掩码，解决静态参数隔离方法无法适应参数重要性时变漂移的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调(SFT)中的参数隔离方法存在静态局限性，假设参数重要性一旦确定便保持不变。本文通过实证研究发现参数重要性在训练过程中存在时间漂移(temporal drift)现象，静态隔离方案无法适应这种动态变化。

Method: 提出Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI)框架：(1)基于梯度的信号在线估计参数重要性；(2)周期性更新隔离掩码，保护新出现的任务关键参数；(3)释放过时参数以恢复模型可塑性，实现动态参数隔离。

Result: 在多个多任务基准测试上，EPI相比静态隔离方法和标准微调方法，持续降低任务干扰和灾难性遗忘，同时提升整体泛化性能。

Conclusion: 参数隔离机制需要与学习多样化能力的演化动态保持同步，动态隔离策略在处理多任务微调时优于静态方案。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) of large language models often suffers from task interference and catastrophic forgetting. Recent approaches alleviate this issue by isolating task-critical parameters during training. However, these methods represent a static solution to a dynamic problem, assuming that parameter importance remains fixed once identified. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that parameter importance exhibits temporal drift over the course of training. To address this, we propose Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI), a fine-tuning framework that adapts isolation decisions based on online estimates of parameter importance. Instead of freezing a fixed subset of parameters, EPI periodically updates isolation masks using gradient-based signals, enabling the model to protect emerging task-critical parameters while releasing outdated ones to recover plasticity. Experiments on diverse multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that EPI consistently reduces interference and forgetting compared to static isolation and standard fine-tuning, while improving overall generalization. Our analysis highlights the necessity of synchronizing isolation mechanisms with the evolving dynamics of learning diverse abilities.

</details>


### [108] [A Complete Symmetry Classification of Shallow ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14037)
*Pranavkrishnan Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对ReLU激活函数的浅层神经网络，利用其不可微性，完成了参数空间对称性的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的参数空间与函数空间不同，不同参数可能实现相同函数（参数对称性），形成的商空间（神经流形）具有丰富的几何性质并影响优化动力学。现有完整分类技术要求激活函数解析，排除了重要的ReLU情形。

Method: 利用ReLU激活函数的不可微性，对浅层神经网络的参数对称性进行理论分析和分类。

Result: 针对ReLU激活函数的浅层神经网络，提供了参数对称性的完整分类。

Conclusion: 通过利用ReLU的不可微特性，成功克服了以往依赖激活函数解析性的限制，为浅层ReLU神经网络的参数对称性分析提供了完整框架。

Abstract: Parameter space is not function space for neural network architectures. This fact, investigated as early as the 1990s under terms such as ``reverse engineering," or ``parameter identifiability", has led to the natural question of parameter space symmetries\textemdash the study of distinct parameters in neural architectures which realize the same function. Indeed, the quotient space obtained by identifying parameters giving rise to the same function, called the \textit{neuromanifold}, has been shown in some cases to have rich geometric properties, impacting optimization dynamics. Thus far, techniques towards complete classifications have required the analyticity of the activation function, notably excising the important case of ReLU. Here, in contrast, we exploit the non-differentiability of the ReLU activation to provide a complete classification of the symmetries in the shallow case.

</details>


### [109] [$π$-Play: Multi-Agent Self-Play via Privileged Self-Distillation without External Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14054)
*Yaocheng Zhang,Yuanheng Zhu,Wenyue Chong,Songjun Tu,Qichao Zhang,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为π-Play的多智能体自进化框架，通过利用自我博弈中自然产生的问题构建路径（QCP）作为特权信息，将传统的稀疏奖励转化为密集反馈，从而在无需标注数据的情况下，显著提升了深度搜索智能体的训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体的训练面临奖励稀疏、信用分配困难和标注数据有限等挑战。传统的自我博弈方法虽然可以减少对数据的依赖，但仅通过稀疏的结果奖励进行优化，导致学习效率低下。作者观察到自我博弈过程中会产生问题构建路径（QCP），这为自蒸馏提供了天然的特权信息来源。

Method: 提出特权信息自我博弈框架（π-Play），这是一个多智能体自进化系统。具体机制为：出题者智能体生成任务及其对应的问题构建路径（QCP）；教师模型利用QCP作为特权上下文，通过自蒸馏对学生模型进行密集监督。该方法将传统的稀疏奖励自我博弈转化为密集反馈的自进化循环。

Result: 实验结果表明，无需标注数据的π-Play方法性能超越了全监督的搜索智能体；与传统的自我博弈方法相比，其进化效率提升了2-3倍。

Conclusion: π-Play成功挖掘并利用了自我博弈过程中产生的特权信息（QCP），解决了传统方法中学习效率低的问题，为深度搜索智能体的训练提供了一种高效、可扩展且无需人工标注数据的新范式。

Abstract: Deep search agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for addressing complex information-seeking tasks, but their training remains challenging due to sparse rewards, weak credit assignment, and limited labeled data. Self-play offers a scalable route to reduce data dependence, but conventional self-play optimizes students only through sparse outcome rewards, leading to low learning efficiency. In this work, we observe that self-play naturally produces a question construction path (QCP) during task generation, an intermediate artifact that captures the reverse solution process. This reveals a new source of privileged information for self-distillation: self-play can itself provide high-quality privileged context for the teacher model in a low-cost and scalable manner, without relying on human feedback or curated privileged information. Leveraging this insight, we propose Privileged Information Self-Play ($π$-Play), a multi-agent self-evolution framework. In $π$-Play, an examiner generates tasks together with their QCPs, and a teacher model leverages QCP as privileged context to densely supervise a student via self-distillation. This design transforms conventional sparse-reward self-play into a dense-feedback self-evolution loop. Extensive experiments show that data-free $π$-Play surpasses fully supervised search agents and improves evolutionary efficiency by 2-3$\times$ over conventional self-play.

</details>


### [110] [Neural architectures for resolving references in program code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14073)
*Gergő Szalay,Gergely Zsolt Kovács,Sándor Teleki,Balázs Pintér,Tibor Gregorics*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对编程语言中的引用重写问题，提出了新的序列到序列架构，在鲁棒性和可扩展性上显著优于基线模型，并将真实反编译任务的错误率降低了42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者受到现实世界中反编译任务（decompilation task）的启发，发现引用解析与重写是编程语言中的基础问题，因此将其抽象为直接索引和间接索引问题进行研究。

Method: 作者首先针对直接和间接索引问题创建了合成基准测试，发现现有的知名序列到序列机器学习架构在这些任务上表现不佳。随后，针对这两个问题分别提出了新的序列到序列架构。

Result: 实验测量表明，新提出的架构在鲁棒性和可扩展性上均优于基线模型，能够处理比最佳基线长十倍的样本。在真实反编译switch语句的任务中，扩展模型将错误率降低了42%。消融研究证实了架构中各组件的必要性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功设计了针对索引问题的新序列到序列架构，显著提升了模型处理长序列的能力，并在实际反编译任务中取得了显著的性能提升，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Resolving and rewriting references is fundamental in programming languages. Motivated by a real-world decompilation task, we abstract reference rewriting into the problems of direct and indirect indexing by permutation. We create synthetic benchmarks for these tasks and show that well-known sequence-to-sequence machine learning architectures are struggling on these benchmarks. We introduce new sequence-to-sequence architectures for both problems. Our measurements show that our architectures outperform the baselines in both robustness and scalability: our models can handle examples that are ten times longer compared to the best baseline. We measure the impact of our architecture in the real-world task of decompiling switch statements, which has an indexing subtask. According to our measurements, the extended model decreases the error rate by 42%. Multiple ablation studies show that all components of our architectures are essential.

</details>


### [111] [Momentum Further Constrains Sharpness at the Edge of Stochastic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14108)
*Arseniy Andreyev,Advikar Ananthkumar,Marc Walden,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 动量SGD在近不稳定边界存在两种依赖批大小的稳定机制：小批次趋向更平坦区域，大批次恢复经典稳定效应趋向更尖锐区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明梯度下降在接近不稳定性边界处自组织，影响优化轨迹和解的性质。动量和迷你批梯度在深度学习优化中被广泛使用，但尚不清楚它们是否在类似的近不稳定区域中运行。

Method: 通过分析带动量的随机梯度下降（SGD）的线性稳定性阈值和批次锐度（Batch Sharpness，即期望方向小批量曲率）的收敛行为，比较不同批次大小下动量对稳定性边界的影响。

Result: 批次锐度在两种机制下趋于不同稳定值：小批次时收敛于较低值2(1−β)/η，动量放大随机波动，偏好比普通SGD更平坦的区域；大批次时收敛于较高值2(1+β)/η，动量恢复经典稳定作用，偏好与全批动力学一致的更尖锐区域。该现象无法用单一动量调整的稳定性阈值解释。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了动量SGD的批次大小依赖性双稳定机制，与线性稳定性阈值一致，对超参数调优和参数耦合具有重要启示，说明动量在小批次和大批次下应采取不同策略。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that (stochastic) gradient descent self-organizes near an instability boundary, shaping both optimization and the solutions found. Momentum and mini-batch gradients are widely used in practical deep learning optimization, but it remains unclear whether they operate in a comparable regime of instability. We demonstrate that SGD with momentum exhibits an Edge of Stochastic Stability (EoSS)-like regime with batch-size-dependent behavior that cannot be explained by a single momentum-adjusted stability threshold. Batch Sharpness (the expected directional mini-batch curvature) stabilizes in two distinct regimes: at small batch sizes it converges to a lower plateau $2(1-β)/η$, reflecting amplification of stochastic fluctuations by momentum and favoring flatter regions than vanilla SGD; at large batch sizes it converges to a higher plateau $2(1+β)/η$, where momentum recovers its classical stabilizing effect and favors sharper regions consistent with full-batch dynamics. We further show that this aligns with linear stability thresholds and discuss the implications for hyperparameter tuning and coupling.

</details>


### [112] [Complex Interpolation of Matrices with an application to Multi-Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14118)
*Adi Arbel,Stefan Steinerberger,Ronen Talmon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了正定矩阵插值的谱性质，发现算子范数的对数线性性与特征向量共享机制相关，并基于此提出了多视图数据的多流形学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个对称正定矩阵之间插值 $A^{1-x} B^x$ 的谱性质，旨在通过插值视角探究矩阵间是否存在“共同结构”（特征向量方向相似），并为多视图数据中识别共同和独特潜在结构提供理论基础。

Method: 对 $0 \leq x \leq 1$ 范围内的矩阵插值 $A^{1-x} B^x$ 进行谱分析，证明算子范数 $\|A^{1-x} B^x\|$ 的严格对数线性性等价于原矩阵存在共享特征向量；通过稳定性分析揭示近似对数线性性如何迫使主奇异向量与两矩阵的主特征向量对齐。

Result: 严格证明了算子范数的对数线性性与特征向量共享的等价关系；建立了稳定性界限，表明近似对数线性性会约束主奇异向量与主特征向量的对齐关系。

Conclusion: 这些发现为多流形学习框架提供了理论支撑，使该框架能够有效识别多视图数据中的共同潜在结构和独特潜在结构。

Abstract: Given two symmetric positive-definite matrices $A, B \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$, we study the spectral properties of the interpolation $A^{1-x} B^x$ for $0 \leq x \leq 1$. The presence of `common structures' in $A$ and $B$, eigenvectors pointing in a similar direction, can be investigated using this interpolation perspective. Generically, exact log-linearity of the operator norm $\|A^{1-x} B^x\|$ is equivalent to the existence of a shared eigenvector in the original matrices; stability bounds show that approximate log-linearity forces principal singular vectors to align with leading eigenvectors of both matrices. These results give rise to and provide theoretical justification for a multi-manifold learning framework that identifies common and distinct latent structures in multiview data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Event Tensor: A Unified Abstraction for Compiling Dynamic Megakernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13327)
*Hongyi Jin,Bohan Hou,Guanjie Wang,Ruihang Lai,Jinqi Chen,Zihao Ye,Yaxing Cai,Yixin Dong,Xinhao Cheng,Zhihao Zhang,Yilong Zhao,Yingyi Huang,Lijie Yang,Jinchen Jiang,Gabriele Oliaro,Jianan Ji,Xupeng Miao,Vinod Grover,Todd C. Mowry,Zhihao Jia,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Event Tensor，一种用于动态megakernel的统一编译器抽象，通过编码分块任务间的依赖关系，实现了对形状动态性和数据依赖动态性的一流支持，基于此构建的编译器ETC在LLM推理服务中达到了最先进的延迟性能并显著降低了系统预热开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU工作负载（特别是大语言模型推理）受限于kernel启动开销和粗粒度同步，限制了kernel间并行性。现有的megakernel技术虽然通过将多个算子融合到单个持久化kernel中来消除启动间隙，但在处理实际工作负载中的动态形状和数据依赖计算方面存在困难。

Method: 提出Event Tensor这一统一编译器抽象，用于编码分块任务之间的依赖关系，为形状动态性和数据依赖动态性提供一等支持。基于该抽象构建Event Tensor Compiler (ETC)，应用静态和动态调度变换来生成高性能的持久化kernel。

Result: 实验评估表明，ETC在LLM服务延迟方面达到了最先进水平，同时显著降低了系统预热开销。

Conclusion: Event Tensor抽象成功解决了动态megakernel中的关键挑战，为动态形状和数据依赖计算提供了有效的编译器支持，在LLM推理服务中展现出优越的性能表现。

Abstract: Modern GPU workloads, especially large language model (LLM) inference, suffer from kernel launch overheads and coarse synchronization that limit inter-kernel parallelism. Recent megakernel techniques fuse multiple operators into a single persistent kernel to eliminate launch gaps and expose inter-kernel parallelism, but struggle to handle dynamic shapes and data-dependent computation in real workloads. We present Event Tensor, a unified compiler abstraction for dynamic megakernels. Event Tensor encodes dependencies between tiled tasks, and enables first-class support for both shape and data-dependent dynamism. Built atop this abstraction, our Event Tensor Compiler (ETC) applies static and dynamic scheduling transformations to generate high-performance persistent kernels. Evaluations show that ETC achieves state-of-the-art LLM serving latency while significantly reducing system warmup overhead.

</details>


### [114] [PackSELL: A Sparse Matrix Format for Precision-Agnostic High-Performance SpMV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13433)
*Kengo Suzuki,Takeshi Iwashita*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PackSELL稀疏矩阵格式，通过增量编码和打包方案优化GPU上的SpMV性能，相比cuSPARSE最高加速1.63倍，在混合精度PCG求解器中加速达2.09倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏矩阵格式在GPU上的稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV）受限于内存占用和数据移动开销，且缺乏对多样化数据表示（如非IEEE格式）的支持。需要一种既能减少内存足迹又能灵活支持不同精度表示的格式来提升性能。

Method: 基于切片ELLPACK（SELL）格式，PackSELL采用两项关键技术创新：1）列索引的增量编码；2）新颖的打包方案，将索引增量-值对存储在单个字中，从而减少内存占用和数据移动。该设计支持对增量和值之间的位分配进行细粒度控制，实现对包括非IEEE格式在内的灵活数据表示的支持。

Result: 在半精度（FP16）配置下，PackSELL的SpMV内核相比cuSPARSE的SELL内核性能提升最高达1.63倍；使用自定义格式配置时，PackSELL在保持FP32级别精度的同时超越FP16 cuSPARSE的性能。在混合精度预条件共轭梯度（PCG）求解器中，PackSELL相比标准全精度PCG实现最高2.09倍的加速。

Conclusion: PackSELL通过增量编码和创新的打包方案有效降低了稀疏矩阵存储的内存占用和数据移动开销，在GPU SpMV及稀疏线性求解器应用中展现出显著的性能优势，同时支持灵活的数据精度配置，为高性能稀疏计算提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: We propose a new sparse matrix format, PackSELL, designed to support diverse data representations and enable efficient sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) on GPUs. Building on sliced ELLPACK (SELL), PackSELL incorporates delta encoding of column indices and a novel packing scheme that stores each index-delta-value pair in a single word, thereby reducing memory footprint and data movement. This design further enables fine-grained control over the bit allocation between deltas and values, allowing flexible data representations, including non-IEEE formats. Experimental results show that, when configured for half precision (FP16), the PackSELL-based SpMV kernel outperforms the cuSPARSE SELL-based kernel by up to $1.63\times$. Moreover, with configurations using customized formats, PackSELL achieves FP32-level accuracy while exceeding the performance of FP16 cuSPARSE. These benefits extend to sparse linear solvers; for example, a mixed-precision preconditioned conjugate gradient (PCG) solver using PackSELL achieves up to a $2.09\times$ speedup over the standard full-precision PCG.

</details>


### [115] [SAKURAONE: An Open Ethernet-Based AI HPC System and Its Observed Workload Dynamics in a Single-Tenant LLM Development Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13600)
*Fumikazu Konishi,Yuuki Tsubouchi,Hirofumi Tsuruta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SAKURAONE高性能计算集群，其在ISC 2025 TOP500中排名第49，是唯一使用完全开放网络栈（800 GbE+SONiC）的百强系统。文章还展示了该集群在单一研究项目中的实际使用模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展示基于开放网络技术构建的大规模GPU集群的可行性与性能表现，并研究在实际研究项目统一调度下GPU集群的资源利用动态特征。

Method: 构建由100个节点（每节点8块NVIDIA H100 GPU）组成的集群，采用轨优化800 GbE叶脊架构与RoCEv2互连，配备2 PB全闪存Lustre文件系统；通过单一研究项目独占使用，收集并分析作业调度数据。

Result: HPL Rmax达33.95 PFLOP/s，HPCG为396.295 TFLOP/s，HPL-MxP FP8达339.86 PFLOP/s；作业特征分析表明小规模作业数量占优，但少数大规模作业消耗大部分GPU时间，且随项目推进资源使用从大规模训练向中等规模迭代优化转移。

Conclusion: SAKURAONE证明了基于供应商中立技术的800 GbE开放网络栈能够支撑顶级HPC系统规模；同时揭示了GPU集群在真实统一项目负载下的资源利用规律，为未来GPU集群的设计与调度优化提供了实证依据。

Abstract: SAKURAONE is a managed high performance computing (HPC) cluster developed and operated by the SAKURA Internet Research Center. It builds on the KOKARYOKU PHY bare metal GPU platform and is optimized for advanced workloads, including large language model (LLM) training. In ISC 2025 TOP500, SAKURAONE is ranked 49th by HPL and is the only top 100 system that uses a fully open networking stack - 800 GbE with SONiC - demonstrating the scalability of vendor-neutral technology. Measured performance is 33.95 PFLOP/s (HPL Rmax), 396.295 TFLOP/s (HPCG), and 339.86 PFLOP/s on HPL-MxP with FP8. The system consists of 100 nodes, each with eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs and a 2 PB all-flash Lustre file system, interconnected via a rail-optimized 800 GbE leaf-spine fabric with RoCEv2. Through exclusive use by a single research project, we observed the characteristics of development-related jobs. Consistent with previous HPC studies, small-scale jobs dominated in number, while a few large-scale jobs accounted for most GPU resource time. As the project progressed, resource use shifted from large-scale to mid-scale jobs, reflecting a transition from initial large-scale training to iterative refinement. These observations illustrate the real-world utilization dynamics of GPU clusters under unified project workloads.

</details>


### [116] [OffloadFS: Leveraging Disaggregated Storage for Computation Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13743)
*Sungho Moon,Daegyu Han,Hera Koo,Sangeun Chae,Duck-Ho Bae,Euiseong Seo,Beomseok Nam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了OffloadFS，一个用户态文件系统，通过将近数据处理技术应用于 disaggregated storage 节点，显著提升了RocksDB和机器学习预处理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 disaggregated storage 系统中，NVMe over fabrics (NVMeoF) 技术虽然提供了丰富的计算和内存资源，但这些资源通常仅用于简单的I/O委托，存在严重的资源浪费问题。如何有效利用这些未被充分利用的资源来提升系统性能成为研究动机。

Method: 提出了OffloadFS，一个用户级文件系统，能够将I/O密集型任务卸载到 disaggregated storage 节点进行近数据处理，也可选择卸载到对等计算节点。该系统采用无分布式锁管理的设计，并通过优化缓存管理减少不同I/O操作线程间的干扰。基于OffloadFS，开发了OffloadDB（用于卸载RocksDB的MemTable flush和compaction操作）和OffloadPrep（用于卸载机器学习图像预处理任务）。

Result: 实验评估显示，相比OCFS2，OffloadFS将RocksDB的性能提升了最高3.36倍，将机器学习预处理任务的性能提升了最高1.85倍。

Conclusion: OffloadFS成功证明了通过近数据处理技术利用 disaggregated storage 节点上未被充分利用的计算和内存资源的可行性，为 disaggregated storage 系统的性能优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Disaggregated storage systems improve resource utilization and enable independent scaling of storage and compute resources by separating storage resources from computing resources in data centers. NVMe over fabrics (NVMeoF) is a key technology that underpins the functionality and benefits of disaggregated storage systems. While NVMeoF inherently possesses substantial computing and memory capacity, these resources are often underutilized for tasks beyond simple I/O delegation. This study proposes OffloadFS, a user-level file system that enables offloaded IO-intensive tasks primarily to a disaggregated storage node for near-data processing, with the option to offload to peer compute nodes as well, without the need for distributed lock management. OffloadFS optimizes cache management by reducing interference between threads performing distinct I/O operations. On top of OffloadFS, we develop OffloadDB, which enables RocksDB to offload MemTable flush and compaction operations, and OffloadPrep, which offloads image pre-processing tasks for machine learning to disaggregated storage nodes. Our evaluation shows that OffloadFS improves the performance of RocksDB and machine learning pre-processing tasks by up to 3.36x and 1.85x, respectively, compared to OCFS2.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Don't Let AI Agents YOLO Your Files: Shifting Information and Control to Filesystems for Agent Safety and Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13536)
*Shawn,Zhong,Junxuan Liao,Jing Liu,Mai Zheng,Andrea C. Arpaci-Dusseau,Remzi H. Arpaci-Dusseau*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出YoloFS，一个面向AI编程代理的原生文件系统，通过暂存、快照和渐进式权限三种技术，在不牺牲自主性的前提下显著提升了文件系统操作的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编程代理在用户文件系统上操作时常导致数据损坏、文件删除和机密泄露等安全问题，当前方法在安全性和自主性之间存在权衡：无限制访问会带来风险，而频繁的权限提示则增加了用户负担并阻碍代理运行。为理解这一问题，作者对13个框架中的290份公开报告进行了首次系统的代理文件系统误用研究，发现现有代理对文件系统影响的信息有限且缺乏足够控制。

Method: 基于将信息和控制权转移到文件系统本身的原则，设计了YoloFS——一个面向代理的原生文件系统，包含三项核心技术：(1) 暂存机制：在提交前隔离所有变更，为用户提供纠正控制；(2) 快照机制：将此控制扩展至代理，使其能检测并纠正自身错误；(3) 渐进式权限：通过最小交互的门控访问为用户提供预防性控制。同时引入了新的评估方法来捕获用户-代理-文件系统交互。

Result: 在11个包含隐藏副作用的任务中，YoloFS在8个任务上实现了代理自我纠正，同时保持所有影响处于暂存和可审查状态；在112个常规任务中，YoloFS在匹配基线成功率的同时需要更少的用户交互。

Conclusion: YoloFS成功解决了AI编程代理在文件系统操作中安全性与自主性的权衡问题，通过将信息和控制转移到文件系统层面，既保护了用户数据安全，又维持了代理的工作效率，为构建更安全的AI代理系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: AI coding agents operate directly on users' filesystems, where they regularly corrupt data, delete files, and leak secrets. Current approaches force a tradeoff between safety and autonomy: unrestricted access risks harm, while frequent permission prompts burden users and block agents. To understand this problem, we conduct the first systematic study of agent filesystem misuse, analyzing 290 public reports across 13 frameworks. Our analysis reveals that today's agents have limited information about their filesystem effects and insufficient control over them. We therefore argue for shifting this information and control to the filesystem itself.
  Based on this principle, we design YoloFS, an agent-native filesystem with three techniques. Staging isolates all mutations before commit, giving users corrective control. Snapshots extend this control to agents, letting them detect and correct their own mistakes. Progressive permission provides users with preventive control by gating access with minimal interaction. To evaluate YoloFS, we introduce a new methodology that captures user-agent-filesystem interactions. On 11 tasks with hidden side effects, YoloFS enables agent self-correction in 8 while keeping all effects staged and reviewable. On 112 routine tasks, YoloFS requires fewer user interactions while matching the baseline success rate.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Exploration and Exploitation Errors Are Measurable for Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13151)
*Jaden Park,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Robert D. Nowak,Kangwook Lee,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可控的评估框架来量化语言模型智能体在决策任务中的探索与利用能力，发现即使是先进的模型也表现不佳，但推理模型和简单的工程优化能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在复杂的开放式决策任务中被广泛应用，但如何在不访问智能体内部策略的情况下，系统地区分和量化其探索与利用能力仍是一个挑战。

Method: 设计受具身AI场景启发的可控环境，包含部分可观测的2D网格地图和未知任务有向无环图(DAG)；通过编程调整地图生成来强调探索或利用难度；设计了与策略无关的度量标准来从智能体行为中量化探索和利用错误。

Result: 评估了多种前沿语言模型智能体，发现即使是最先进的模型也难以完成任务，不同模型表现出不同的失败模式；推理模型能更有效地解决问题，且通过最小的工程优化可以显著改善探索和利用能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了量化语言模型智能体探索与利用能力的评估框架，揭示了当前模型的局限性，并展示了推理模型和工程优化的潜力，为未来智能体改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Language Model (LM) agents are increasingly used in complex open-ended decision-making tasks, from AI coding to physical AI. A core requirement in these settings is the ability to both explore the problem space and exploit acquired knowledge effectively. However, systematically distinguishing and quantifying exploration and exploitation from observed actions without access to the agent's internal policy remains challenging. To address this, we design controllable environments inspired by practical embodied AI scenarios. Each environment consists of a partially observable 2D grid map and an unknown task Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). The map generation can be programmatically adjusted to emphasize exploration or exploitation difficulty. To enable policy-agnostic evaluation, we design a metric to quantify exploration and exploitation errors from agent's actions. We evaluate a variety of frontier LM agents and find that even state-of-the-art models struggle on our task, with different models exhibiting distinct failure modes. We further observe that reasoning models solve the task more effectively and show both exploration and exploitation can be significantly improved through minimal harness engineering. We release our code \href{https://github.com/jjj-madison/measurable-explore-exploit}{here}.

</details>


### [119] [SciFi: A Safe, Lightweight, User-Friendly, and Fully Autonomous Agentic AI Workflow for Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13180)
*Qibin Liu,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个安全、轻量且用户友好的智能体框架，用于自主执行明确的科学任务，通过隔离执行环境、三层智能体循环和自我评估的“执行-直到”机制，在有效利用不同能力水平大语言模型的同时，确保了安全可靠的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体AI的最新进展使得日益自主的工作流成为可能，但现有系统在实现真实世界科学研究中的可靠部署方面仍面临重大挑战，缺乏安全性和可靠性。

Method: 提出了一个结合三大核心组件的框架：1) 隔离的执行环境以确保安全；2) 三层智能体循环结构；3) 自我评估的“执行-直到”（do-until）机制。该框架专注于具有明确上下文和停止标准的结构化任务。

Result: 该框架支持端到端的自动化，仅需极少的人工干预，能够有效利用不同能力水平的大语言模型，并确保操作的安全性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架使研究人员能够卸载常规工作负载，将更多精力投入到创造性活动和开放式科学探究中，实现了安全自主的科学任务执行。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI have enabled increasingly autonomous workflows, but existing systems still face substantial challenges in achieving reliable deployment in real-world scientific research. In this work, we present a safe, lightweight, and user-friendly agentic framework for the autonomous execution of well-defined scientific tasks. The framework combines an isolated execution environment, a three-layer agent loop, and a self-assessing do-until mechanism to ensure safe and reliable operation while effectively leveraging large language models of varying capability levels. By focusing on structured tasks with clearly defined context and stopping criteria, the framework supports end-to-end automation with minimal human intervention, enabling researchers to offload routine workloads and devote more effort to creative activities and open-ended scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [120] [Numerical Instability and Chaos: Quantifying the Unpredictability of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13206)
*Chashi Mahiul Islam,Alan Villarreal,Mao Nishino,Shaeke Salman,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM数值不稳定性源于浮点精度误差，发现了早期层的混沌“雪崩效应”和三种普适的动力学行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛集成到智能体工作流中，数值不稳定性导致的不可预测性已成为关键的可靠性问题。现有研究虽已证实其下游影响，但对根本原因和底层机制的理解仍不充分。

Method: 通过严格分析浮点表示的有限数值精度，追踪舍入误差在Transformer计算层中的传播、放大或消散过程。重点研究早期层的混沌动力学行为，并进行跨多数据集和模型架构的广泛验证。

Result: 发现了早期层的混沌“雪崩效应”，即微小扰动会触发二元结果（快速放大或完全衰减）。证明了LLM表现出普适的、依赖尺度的混沌行为，具有三种不同模式：稳定态（扰动低于阈值并消失）、混沌态（舍入误差主导并驱动输出发散）、信号主导态（真实输入变化覆盖数值噪声）。

Conclusion: LLM的不可预测性根植于浮点数的有限精度，其数值误差传播表现出普适的混沌动力学特征。这一发现为理解LLM的可靠性问题提供了理论基础，对改进模型设计和部署具有指导意义。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into agentic workflows, their unpredictability stemming from numerical instability has emerged as a critical reliability issue. While recent studies have demonstrated the significant downstream effects of these instabilities, the root causes and underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this paper, we present a rigorous analysis of how unpredictability is rooted in the finite numerical precision of floating-point representations, tracking how rounding errors propagate, amplify, or dissipate through Transformer computation layers. Specifically, we identify a chaotic "avalanche effect" in the early layers, where minor perturbations trigger binary outcomes: either rapid amplification or complete attenuation. Beyond specific error instances, we demonstrate that LLMs exhibit universal, scale-dependent chaotic behaviors characterized by three distinct regimes: 1) a stable regime, where perturbations fall below an input-dependent threshold and vanish, resulting in constant outputs; 2) a chaotic regime, where rounding errors dominate and drive output divergence; and 3) a signal-dominated regime, where true input variations override numerical noise. We validate these findings extensively across multiple datasets and model architectures.

</details>


### [121] [Optimizing Earth Observation Satellite Schedules under Unknown Operational Constraints: An Active Constraint Acquisition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13283)
*Mohamed-Bachir Belaid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对地球观测卫星调度问题中约束条件未知（仅能通过二进制预言机判断可行性）的实际场景，提出了一种保守约束获取方法（CCA）并将其嵌入Learn&Optimize框架。实验表明该方法能显著减少预言机查询次数，相比基线方法将最优性差距从65-68%降至17.7-35.8%，且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地球观测卫星调度方法通常假设操作约束模型是完全已知的，但在实际工程实践中，观测分离、电力预算和热限制等约束条件往往嵌入在工程制品或高保真仿真器中，而非以显式数学模型形式存在。这促使研究者关注约束未知的调度问题场景，其中优化目标已知但可行性需通过交互式查询学习。

Method: 本文针对简化的成对分离约束和全局容量约束模型，提出了保守约束获取（CCA）方法，这是一种领域特定的约束识别过程，旨在高效识别合理约束同时避免对学习模型进行不必要的过度收紧。该方法被嵌入Learn&Optimize（L&O）框架中，通过交替进行约束模型下的优化和针对性的预言机查询来支持交互式搜索过程。

Result: 在包含多达50个任务和密集约束网络的合成实例上，L&O方法相比无知识的贪婪基线有显著改进，且比两阶段先获取后求解基线（FAO）使用更少的主预言机查询。对于n≤30的情况，平均差距从65-68%（优先贪婪）降至17.7-35.8%（L&O）。在n=50时，L&O相比FAO平均差距更优（17.9% vs 20.3%），同时仅需21.3次主查询（vs 100次）且执行时间减少约5倍。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了地球观测卫星调度中约束未知的实际问题，证明了通过保守约束获取与交互式优化相结合的方法能够有效学习隐式约束。该方法在保持较优求解质量的同时，显著降低了计算开销和预言机查询成本，为处理实际工程中约束不明确的复杂优化问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) satellite scheduling (deciding which imaging tasks to perform and when) is a well-studied combinatorial optimization problem. Existing methods typically assume that the operational constraint model is fully specified in advance. In practice, however, constraints governing separation between observations, power budgets, and thermal limits are often embedded in engineering artefacts or high-fidelity simulators rather than in explicit mathematical models. We study EO scheduling under \emph{unknown constraints}: the objective is known, but feasibility must be learned interactively from a binary oracle. Working with a simplified model restricted to pairwise separation and global capacity constraints, we introduce Conservative Constraint Acquisition~(CCA), a domain-specific procedure designed to identify justified constraints efficiently in practice while limiting unnecessary tightening of the learned model. Embedded in the \textsc{Learn\&Optimize} framework, CCA supports an interactive search process that alternates optimization under a learned constraint model with targeted oracle queries. On synthetic instances with up to 50~tasks and dense constraint networks, L\&O improves over a no-knowledge greedy baseline and uses far fewer main oracle queries than a two-phase acquire-then-solve baseline (FAO). For $n\leq 30$, the average gap drops from 65--68\% (Priority Greedy) to 17.7--35.8\% using L\&O. At $n{=}50$, where the CP-SAT reference is the best feasible solution found in 120~s, L\&O improves on FAO on average (17.9\% vs.\ 20.3\%) while using 21.3 main queries instead of 100 and about $5\times$ less execution time.

</details>


### [122] [WebXSkill: Skill Learning for Autonomous Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13318)
*Zhaoyang Wang,Qianhui Wu,Xuchao Zhang,Chaoyun Zhang,Wenlin Yao,Fazle Elahi Faisal,Baolin Peng,Si Qin,Suman Nath,Qingwei Lin,Chetan Bansal,Dongmei Zhang,Saravan Rajmohan,Jianfeng Gao,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WebXSkill框架，通过引入可执行技能（结合参数化动作程序与步骤级自然语言指导）弥合现有技能表示中的基础差距，在WebArena和WebVoyager基准上分别提升任务成功率最高9.8和12.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主网络代理在长周期工作流中表现不佳，核心瓶颈在于技能表示的基础差距：文本工作流技能提供自然语言指导但无法直接执行，而代码技能虽可执行但对代理不透明，无法提供步骤级理解用于错误恢复或自适应。

Method: WebXSkill框架包含三个阶段：1）技能提取，从合成代理轨迹中挖掘可复用的动作子序列并抽象为参数化技能；2）技能组织，将技能索引为基于URL的图结构以实现上下文感知检索；3）技能部署，提供两种互补模式——用于全自动多步执行的基座模式和作为步骤指令供代理原生规划遵循的引导模式。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager两个基准测试集上，WebXSkill相比基线方法分别提升了最高9.8和12.9个百分点的任务成功率，验证了可执行技能对网络代理的有效性。

Conclusion: 可执行技能成功弥合了文本指导与代码执行之间的差距，使网络代理既能直接执行技能又能进行自适应调整，为长周期工作流任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous web agents powered by large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in completing complex browser tasks, yet they still struggle with long-horizon workflows. A key bottleneck is the grounding gap in existing skill formulations: textual workflow skills provide natural language guidance but cannot be directly executed, while code-based skills are executable but opaque to the agent, offering no step-level understanding for error recovery or adaptation. We introduce WebXSkill, a framework that bridges this gap with executable skills, each pairing a parameterized action program with step-level natural language guidance, enabling both direct execution and agent-driven adaptation. WebXSkill operates in three stages: skill extraction mines reusable action subsequences from readily available synthetic agent trajectories and abstracts them into parameterized skills, skill organization indexes skills into a URL-based graph for context-aware retrieval, and skill deployment exposes two complementary modes, grounded mode for fully automated multi-step execution and guided mode where skills serve as step-by-step instructions that the agent follows with its native planning. On WebArena and WebVoyager, WebXSkill improves task success rate by up to 9.8 and 12.9 points over the baseline, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of executable skills for web agents. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/WebXSkill.

</details>


### [123] [Modality-Native Routing in Agent-to-Agent Networks: A Multimodal A2A Protocol Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12213)
*Vasundra Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在智能体网络中，原生多模态路由相比文本瓶颈基线可提升20个百分点任务准确率，但前提是下游推理智能体必须具备利用丰富上下文的能力；研究提出的MMA2A架构实现了52%的任务完成准确率（对比基线32%），代价是1.8倍延迟增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体间通信通常采用文本瓶颈模式，导致跨模态推理中多模态信号丢失或失真。研究者旨在探究保留原生多模态信号是否能提升任务准确率，以及这一提升是否需要其他条件配合。

Method: 提出MMA2A架构层，通过检查Agent Card能力声明，将语音、图像和文本按原生模态进行路由。研究设计了CrossModal-CS基准测试（50个任务），严格控制LLM后端和任务一致，仅改变路由路径，并进行了消融实验（用关键词匹配替代LLM推理）。

Result: MMA2A实现52%任务完成准确率，文本瓶颈基线为32%（95%置信区间[8,32]pp，McNemar精确p=0.006）。视觉相关任务提升显著：产品缺陷报告+38.5pp，视觉故障排查+16.7pp。消融实验显示，当用关键词匹配替代LLM推理时，准确率差距完全消失（36% vs 36%）。延迟成本为1.8倍。

Conclusion: 路由是多智能体系统中的一阶设计变量，决定了下游推理可用的信息。但仅保留多模态信号是必要而非充分条件，必须同时满足两个层面：协议层路由需与具备足够能力的智能体层推理能力相配合，才能实现准确率提升。

Abstract: Preserving multimodal signals across agent boundaries is necessary for accurate cross-modal reasoning, but it is not sufficient. We show that modality-native routing in Agent-to-Agent (A2A) networks improves task accuracy by 20 percentage points over text-bottleneck baselines, but only when the downstream reasoning agent can exploit the richer context that native routing preserves. An ablation replacing LLM-backed reasoning with keyword matching eliminates the accuracy gap entirely (36% vs. 36%), establishing a two-layer requirement: protocol-level routing must be paired with capable agent-level reasoning for the benefit to materialize.
  We present MMA2A, an architecture layer atop A2A that inspects Agent Card capability declarations to route voice, image, and text parts in their native modality. On CrossModal-CS, a controlled 50-task benchmark with the same LLM backend, same tasks, and only the routing path varying, MMA2A achieves 52% task completion accuracy versus 32% for the text-bottleneck baseline (95% bootstrap CI on $Δ$TCA: [8, 32] pp; McNemar's exact $p = 0.006$). Gains concentrate on vision-dependent tasks: product defect reports improve by +38.5 pp and visual troubleshooting by +16.7 pp. This accuracy gain comes at a $1.8\times$ latency cost from native multimodal processing. These results suggest that routing is a first-order design variable in multi-agent systems, as it determines the information available for downstream reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [Listening Alone, Understanding Together: Collaborative Context Recovery for Privacy-Aware AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13348)
*Tanmay Srivastava,Amartya Basu,Shubham Jain,Vaishnavi Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CONCORD，一个隐私感知的异步助手间协作（A2A）框架。通过实时说话人验证实现仅限主人的语音捕获，并结合时空上下文解析、信息缺口检测和关系感知披露机制，在保护隐私的前提下安全地恢复对话上下文，解决了主动式AI助手在社会部署中的隐私挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI助手从被动响应转向主动“始终监听”模式，面临核心隐私风险：可能捕获未同意说话人的语音，这成为其在社会场景中部署的主要障碍。现有方法难以平衡主动服务与隐私保护。

Method: CONCORD框架包含三个核心机制：(1)实时说话人验证，强制仅捕获主人语音，生成单侧转录；(2)时空上下文解析与信息缺口检测，识别缺失的关键上下文；(3)基于关系感知披露的最小化A2A查询，将上下文恢复建模为助手间协商的安全交换，而非易产生幻觉的推断。

Result: 在多领域对话数据集上，CONCORD实现了91.4%的信息缺口检测召回率、96%的关系分类准确率，以及在隐私敏感披露决策中97%的真阴性率，有效平衡了上下文恢复能力与隐私保护。

Conclusion: 通过将始终监听AI重构为隐私保护代理间的协调问题，CONCORD为主动式会话代理的社会可部署性提供了一条实用路径，证明了隐私保护与上下文理解可以协同实现。

Abstract: We introduce CONCORD, a privacy-aware asynchronous assistant-to-assistant (A2A) framework that leverages collaboration between proactive speech-based AI. As agents evolve from reactive to always-listening assistants, they face a core privacy risk (of capturing non-consenting speakers), which makes their social deployment a challenge. To overcome this, we implement CONCORD, which enforces owner-only speech capture via real-time speaker verification, producing a one-sided transcript that incurs missing context but preserves privacy. We demonstrate that CONCORD can safely recover necessary context through (1) spatio-temporal context resolution, (2) information gap detection, and (3) minimal A2A queries governed by a relationship-aware disclosure. Instead of hallucination-prone inferring, CONCORD treats context recovery as a negotiated safe exchange between assistants. Across a multi-domain dialogue dataset, CONCORD achieves 91.4% recall in gap detection, 96% relationship classification accuracy, and 97% true negative rate in privacy-sensitive disclosure decisions. By reframing always-listening AI as a coordination problem between privacy-preserving agents, CONCORD offers a practical path toward socially deployable proactive conversational agents.

</details>


### [125] [ReSS: Learning Reasoning Models for Tabular Data Prediction via Symbolic Scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13392)
*Chenlang Yi,Gang Li,Zizhan Xiong,Tue Minh Cao,Yanmin Gong,My T. Thai,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ReSS框架，通过决策树提取符号支架指导LLM生成可解释推理，在医疗和金融表格数据预测任务上提升准确率10%并保证推理忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在高风险领域（如医疗和金融）应用广泛，现有符号模型虽具可验证性但缺乏语义表达能力，而通用大语言模型需要专门微调才能掌握领域特定的表格推理，且存在可扩展数据策划和推理一致性双重挑战。

Method: 提出ReSS框架，利用决策树模型提取实例级决策路径作为符号支架，结合输入特征和标签引导LLM生成严格遵循底层决策逻辑的基于自然语言的推理，构建高质量数据集微调预训练LLM，并引入支架不变数据增强策略提升泛化性和可解释性。同时提出幻觉率、解释必要性、解释充分性等量化指标评估忠实度。

Result: 在医疗和金融基准测试中，ReSS训练的模型相比传统决策树和标准微调方法准确率提升高达10%，同时生成忠实且一致的推理，有效降低了幻觉率。

Conclusion: ReSS框架成功桥接符号推理与神经推理，通过符号支架指导LLM生成高质量推理数据，为高风险领域的表格数据预测提供了既准确又具备可解释性的解决方案，量化评估指标证实了方法的忠实性。

Abstract: Tabular data remains prevalent in high-stakes domains such as healthcare and finance, where predictive models are expected to provide both high accuracy and faithful, human-understandable reasoning. While symbolic models offer verifiable logic, they lack semantic expressiveness. Meanwhile, general-purpose LLMs often require specialized fine-tuning to master domain-specific tabular reasoning. To address the dual challenges of scalable data curation and reasoning consistency, we propose ReSS, a systematic framework that bridges symbolic and neural reasoning models. ReSS leverages a decision-tree model to extract instance-level decision paths as symbolic scaffolds. These scaffolds, alongside input features and labels, guide an LLM to generate grounded natural-language reasoning that strictly adheres to the underlying decision logic. The resulting high-quality dataset is used to fine-tune a pretrained LLM into a specialized tabular reasoning model, further enhanced by a scaffold-invariant data augmentation strategy to improve generalization and explainability. To rigorously assess faithfulness, we introduce quantitative metrics including hallucination rate, explanation necessity, and explanation sufficiency. Experimental results on medical and financial benchmarks demonstrate that ReSS-trained models improve traditional decision trees and standard fine-tuning approaches up to $10\%$ while producing faithful and consistent reasoning

</details>


### [126] [Quantifying and Understanding Uncertainty in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13395)
*Yangyi Li,Chenxu Zhao,Mengdi Huai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种带有统计保证的大推理模型不确定性量化方法，并利用Shapley值构建可解释框架识别导致不确定性的训练样本和推理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM不确定性量化方法缺乏有限样本保证；传统共形预测方法忽视推理轨迹与最终答案的逻辑关联；难以在区分推理质量与答案正确性的同时提供理论保证；缺乏对不确定性覆盖来源的可解释性分析。

Method: 提出一种新的共形预测方法，针对“推理-答案”结构进行不确定性量化，提供统计保证；构建统一的样本到步骤解释框架，利用Shapley值识别对保证有充分贡献的训练样本子集及其关键推理步骤；提供理论分析。

Result: 理论证明所提方法具有统计保证；在挑战性推理数据集上的广泛实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了LRM中带有统计保证的不确定性量化与可解释性，弥补了现有方法的不足。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have recently demonstrated significant improvements in complex reasoning. While quantifying generation uncertainty in LRMs is crucial, traditional methods are often insufficient because they do not provide finite-sample guarantees for reasoning-answer generation. Conformal prediction (CP) stands out as a distribution-free and model-agnostic methodology that constructs statistically rigorous uncertainty sets. However, existing CP methods ignore the logical connection between the reasoning trace and the final answer. Additionally, prior studies fail to interpret the origins of uncertainty coverage for LRMs as they typically overlook the specific training factors driving valid reasoning. Notably, it is challenging to disentangle reasoning quality from answer correctness when quantifying uncertainty, while simultaneously establishing theoretical guarantees for computationally efficient explanation methods. To address these challenges, we first propose a novel methodology that quantifies uncertainty in the reasoning-answer structure with statistical guarantees. Subsequently, we develop a unified example-to-step explanation framework using Shapley values that identifies a provably sufficient subset of training examples and their key reasoning steps to preserve the guarantees. We also provide theoretical analyses of our proposed methods. Extensive experiments on challenging reasoning datasets verify the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Scalable Lightweight GUI Agents via Multi-role Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13488)
*Ziwei Wang,Junjie Zheng,Leyang Yang,Sheng Zhou,Xiaoxuan Tang,Zhouhua Fang,Zhiwei Liu,Dajun Chen,Yong Li,Jiajun Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LAMO框架，通过角色导向数据合成与两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习），赋予轻量级MLLM GUI专用知识和任务扩展能力，开发出支持单体执行和多智能体编排的LAMO-3B模型，在资源受限设备上实现高效GUI自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的GUI智能体存在部署成本高昂的问题，轻量级模型在复杂场景下能力受限且任务扩展性差，端到端学习难以适应多智能体系统，而训练多个专家模型成本过高，需要在成本与扩展性之间寻求平衡。

Method: LAMO框架包含两个核心组件：(1)角色导向数据合成；(2)两阶段训练策略：第一阶段采用困惑度加权交叉熵优化的监督微调实现知识蒸馏和视觉感知增强，第二阶段通过强化学习进行角色导向协作探索，从而赋予轻量级MLLM多角色编排能力。

Result: 开发了LAMO-3B模型（3B参数），作为任务可扩展的原生GUI智能体，支持单体执行和多智能体系统(MAS)编排；可作为即插即用的策略执行器与高级规划器配合，持续受益于规划器的进步，实现更高性能上限。大量静态和在线评估验证了设计的有效性。

Conclusion: LAMO框架成功解决了轻量级GUI智能体的成本-扩展性困境，使资源受限设备能够部署具备多角色协作能力的GUI自动化系统，为实际GUI工作流提供了实用化的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) enable digital automation on end-user devices. While scaling both parameters and data has yielded substantial gains, advanced methods still suffer from prohibitive deployment costs on resource-constrained devices. When facing complex in-the-wild scenarios, lightweight GUI agents are bottlenecked by limited capacity and poor task scalability under end-to-end episodic learning, impeding adaptation to multi-agent systems (MAS), while training multiple skill-specific experts remains costly. Can we strike an effective trade-off in this cost-scalability dilemma, enabling lightweight MLLMs to participate in realistic GUI workflows? To address these challenges, we propose the LAMO framework, which endows a lightweight MLLM with GUI-specific knowledge and task scalability, allowing multi-role orchestration to expand its capability boundary for GUI automation. LAMO combines role-oriented data synthesis with a two-stage training recipe: (i) supervised fine-tuning with Perplexity-Weighted Cross-Entropy optimization for knowledge distillation and visual perception enhancement, and (ii) reinforcement learning for role-oriented cooperative exploration. With LAMO, we develop a task-scalable native GUI agent, LAMO-3B, supporting monolithic execution and MAS-style orchestration. When paired with advanced planners as a plug-and-play policy executor, LAMO-3B can continuously benefit from planner advances, enabling a higher performance ceiling. Extensive static and online evaluations validate the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [128] [RiskWebWorld: A Realistic Interactive Benchmark for GUI Agents in E-commerce Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13531)
*Renqi Chen,Zeyin Tao,Jianming Guo,Jing Wang,Zezhou Xu,Jingzhe Zhu,Qingqing Sun,Tianyi Zhang,Shuai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发布了首个电商风控领域的GUI智能体评测基准RiskWebWorld，发现通用大模型表现远优于专用GUI模型，并验证了通过智能体强化学习可提升开源模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI智能体评测基准主要针对良性的、可预测的消费环境，缺乏对高风险、调查性领域（如真实电商风险管理）的评估，因此无法有效衡量智能体在专业环境下的实际能力。

Method: 构建了RiskWebWorld基准，包含来自8个核心领域的1,513个生产级风控任务，模拟了在非合作网站上进行风险操作的真实挑战（包括环境劫持）。同时开发了符合Gymnasium标准的评测基础设施，将策略规划与环境机制解耦，以支持规模化评估和智能体强化学习。

Result: 评测结果显示巨大的能力差距：顶级通用模型成功率为49.1%，而专用开源GUI模型几乎完全失败。这表明在长周期专业任务中，基础模型的规模比零样本界面定位能力更为关键。此外，智能体强化学习将开源模型的性能提升了16.2%。

Conclusion: RiskWebWorld为开发鲁棒的数字工作者提供了一个实用的测试平台，揭示了当前GUI智能体在专业高风险领域的局限性，并验证了通过强化学习提升模型能力的可行性。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents show strong capabilities for automating web tasks, but existing interactive benchmarks primarily target benign, predictable consumer environments. Their effectiveness in high-stakes, investigative domains such as authentic e-commerce risk management remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present RiskWebWorld, the first highly realistic interactive benchmark for evaluating GUI agents in e-commerce risk management. RiskWebWorld features 1,513 tasks sourced from production risk-control pipelines across 8 core domains, and captures the authentic challenges of risk operations on uncooperative websites, partially environmental hijackments. To support scalable evaluation and agentic reinforcement learning (RL), we further build a Gymnasium-compliant infrastructure that decouples policy planning from environment mechanics. Our evaluation across diverse models reveals a dramatic capability gap: top-tier generalist models achieve 49.1% success, while specialized open-weights GUI models lag at near-total failure. This highlights that foundation model scale currently matters more than zero-shot interface grounding in long-horizon professional tasks. We also demonstrate the viability of our infrastructure through agentic RL, which improves open-source models by 16.2%. These results position RiskWebWorld as a practical testbed for developing robust digital workers.

</details>


### [129] [Weight Patching: Toward Source-Level Mechanistic Localization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13694)
*Chenghao Sun,Chengsheng Zhang,Guanzheng Qin,Rui Dai,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出权重修补方法，通过参数空间干预定位模型行为的能力来源，揭示了从浅层载体到执行电路的层级结构，并指导机制感知的模型合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活空间定位和因果追踪方法存在局限性：在激活空间中看似重要的模块可能仅聚合或放大上游信号，而非在其自身参数中编码目标能力。因此需要一种参数空间干预方法来准确定位模型行为的真实来源。

Method: 提出权重修补方法，这是一种面向源头的参数空间干预方法。该方法针对架构相同但在目标能力表达强度上不同的配对模型，将特化模型的选定模块权重替换到基础模型中。研究在指令遵循任务上实例化该方法，并引入以向量锚点行为界面为中心的框架，为开放生成中任务相关控制状态的形成或恢复提供共享内部标准。

Result: 分析揭示了从浅层候选源端载体到聚合路由模块，再到下游执行电路的层级结构。恢复的组件分数还可指导机制感知的模型合并，在所评估的专家组合中改进选择性融合，并提供额外的外部验证。

Conclusion: 权重修补方法成功解决了激活空间方法的局限性，实现了参数空间层面的行为定位，揭示了模型能力的层级组织结构，并为机制感知的模型合并提供了有效指导，为机械可解释性研究提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks to localize model behavior to the internal components that causally realize it. Prior work has advanced activation-space localization and causal tracing, but modules that appear important in activation space may merely aggregate or amplify upstream signals rather than encode the target capability in their own parameters. To address this gap, we propose Weight Patching, a parameter-space intervention method for source-oriented analysis in paired same-architecture models that differ in how strongly they express a target capability under the inputs of interest. Given a base model and a behavior-specialized counterpart, Weight Patching replaces selected module weights from the specialized model into the base model under a fixed input. We instantiate the method on instruction following and introduce a framework centered on a vector-anchor behavioral interface that provides a shared internal criterion for whether a task-relevant control state has been formed or recovered in open-ended generation. Under this framework, the analysis reveals a hierarchy from shallow candidate source-side carriers to aggregation and routing modules, and further to downstream execution circuits. The recovered component scores can also guide mechanism-aware model merging, improving selective fusion across the evaluated expert combinations and providing additional external validation.

</details>


### [130] [Rethinking AI Hardware: A Three-Layer Cognitive Architecture for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13757)
*Li Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tri-Spirit三层认知架构，通过将智能分解为规划、推理和执行并映射到不同硬件，相比基线方法减少75.6%延迟和71.1%能耗，同时降低30%的LLM调用并实现77.6%离线任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范式（云中心AI、设备端推理、边缘-云流水线）将规划、推理和执行视为单一流程，导致不必要的延迟、能耗增加和行为连续性碎片化问题。下一代自主AI系统不仅受限于模型能力，还受限于异构硬件上智能的结构化方式。

Method: 提出Tri-Spirit架构：三层认知框架将智能分解为规划层（Super Layer）、推理层（Agent Layer）和执行层（Reflex Layer），每层映射到不同计算基板并通过异步消息总线协调。系统包含参数化路由策略、习惯编译机制（将重复推理路径提升为零推理执行策略）、收敛记忆模型和显式安全约束。

Result: 在2000个合成任务的可复现仿真中评估，相比云中心和边缘基线：平均任务延迟降低75.6%，能耗降低71.1%，LLM调用减少30%，实现77.6%离线任务完成率。

Conclusion: 认知分解（而非单纯的模型缩放）是AI硬件系统级效率的主要驱动力，为下一代自主AI系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: The next generation of autonomous AI systems will be constrained not only by model capability, but by how intelligence is structured across heterogeneous hardware. Current paradigms -- cloud-centric AI, on-device inference, and edge-cloud pipelines -- treat planning, reasoning, and execution as a monolithic process, leading to unnecessary latency, energy consumption, and fragmented behavioral continuity. We introduce the Tri-Spirit Architecture, a three-layer cognitive framework that decomposes intelligence into planning (Super Layer), reasoning (Agent Layer), and execution (Reflex Layer), each mapped to distinct compute substrates and coordinated via an asynchronous message bus. We formalize the system with a parameterized routing policy, a habit-compilation mechanism that promotes repeated reasoning paths into zero-inference execution policies, a convergent memory model, and explicit safety constraints. We evaluate the architecture in a reproducible simulation of 2000 synthetic tasks against cloud-centric and edge-only baselines. Tri-Spirit reduces mean task latency by 75.6 percent and energy consumption by 71.1 percent, while decreasing LLM invocations by 30 percent and enabling 77.6 percent offline task completion. These results suggest that cognitive decomposition, rather than model scaling alone, is a primary driver of system-level efficiency in AI hardware.

</details>


### [131] [The cognitive companion: a lightweight parallel monitoring architecture for detecting and recovering from reasoning degradation in LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13759)
*Rafflesia Khan,Nafiul Islam Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cognitive Companion并行监控架构，包含LLM型和Probe型两种实现，用于缓解大语言模型在多步任务中的推理退化问题。实验表明该方法能减少52-62%的循环错误，Probe型实现零推理开销，但效果具有任务类型依赖性且可能存在模型规模下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多步任务中存在严重的推理退化问题，包括循环、漂移和卡死状态，在困难任务上失败率高达30%。现有解决方案存在明显缺陷：硬性步骤限制过于粗暴，而基于LLM-as-judge的监控每步会产生10-15%的开销。因此需要一种既能有效监控又不会显著增加推理成本的方法。

Method: 提出Cognitive Companion并行监控架构，包含两种实现：(1) LLM-based Companion：使用语言模型进行监控；(2) Probe-based Companion：基于模型第28层隐藏状态训练探测器，实现零推理开销的监控。研究进行了三批次可行性实验，主要基于Gemma 4 E4B模型，并扩展探索了Qwen 2.5 1.5B和Llama 3.2 1B等小规模模型。

Result: LLM-based Companion在易循环任务上减少52-62%的重复，开销约11%；Probe-based Companion平均效应量+0.471，零推理开销，最强探测器的交叉验证AUROC达0.840。关键发现：效果具有任务类型依赖性——对易循环和开放任务有益，对结构化任务效果中性或负面；小模型(1B-1.5B)实验显示存在规模下限，Companion未能提升性能。

Conclusion: 本研究作为可行性研究而非最终验证，提供了积极证据表明子词元监控可能有效。研究识别出任务类型敏感性作为实际设计约束，建议未来研究可探索选择性激活Companion的方向，即根据任务类型动态决定是否启用监控机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents on multi-step tasks suffer reasoning degradation, looping, drift, stuck states, at rates up to 30% on hard tasks. Current solutions include hard step limits (abrupt) or LLM-as-judge monitoring (10-15% overhead per step). This paper introduces the Cognitive Companion, a parallel monitoring architecture with two implementations: an LLM-based Companion and a novel zero-overhead Probe-based Companion. We report a three-batch feasibility study centered on Gemma 4 E4B, with an additional exploratory small-model analysis on Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Llama 3.2 1B. In our experiments, the LLM-based Companion reduced repetition on loop-prone tasks by 52-62% with approximately 11% overhead. The Probe-based Companion, trained on hidden states from layer 28, showed a mean effect size of +0.471 at zero measured inference overhead; its strongest probe result achieved cross-validated AUROC 0.840 on a small proxy-labeled dataset. A key empirical finding is that companion benefit appears task-type dependent: companions are most helpful on loop-prone and open-ended tasks, while effects are neutral or negative on more structured tasks. Our small-model experiments also suggest a possible scale boundary: companions did not improve the measured quality proxy on 1B-1.5B models, even when interventions fired. Overall, the paper should be read as a feasibility study rather than a definitive validation. The results provide encouraging evidence that sub-token monitoring may be useful, identify task-type sensitivity as a practical design constraint, and motivate selective companion activation as a promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [132] [AlphaCNOT: Learning CNOT Minimization with Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13812)
*Jacopo Cossio,Daniele Lizzio Bosco,Riccardo Romanello,Giuseppe Serra,Carla Piazza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlphaCNOT，一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)的强化学习框架，通过将CNOT门最小化建模为规划问题，相比现有方法实现了最高32%的门数量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前噪声中等规模量子(NISQ)设备存在误差传播问题，其规模通常与操作数量成正比。CNOT门作为Clifford+T集中唯一的双量子比特门，其最小化对量子电路优化至关重要。现有启发式算法（如PMH）和基于强化学习的方法仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出AlphaCNOT框架，采用基于模型的强化学习方法，将CNOT门最小化问题建模为规划问题。核心是结合蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)，利用前瞻搜索评估未来轨迹，从而找到更高效的CNOT序列。方法涵盖无拓扑约束的线性可逆合成和有拓扑约束的合成两种场景。

Result: 在线性可逆合成任务中，相比PMH基线实现了高达32%的CNOT门数量减少；在有拓扑约束的版本中，针对多种拓扑结构（最多8量子比特），相比最先进的基于RL的解决方案均获得一致的门数量减少。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，强化学习与基于搜索的策略相结合可有效应用于不同的电路优化任务（如Clifford最小化），有助于推动向"量子实用化"时代的过渡。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimization is a central task in Quantum Computing, as current Noisy Intermediate Scale Quantum devices suffer from error propagation that often scales with the number of operations. Among quantum operations, the CNOT gate is of fundamental importance, being the only 2-qubit gate in the universal Clifford+T set. The problem of CNOT gates minimization has been addressed by heuristic algorithms such as the well-known Patel-Markov-Hayes (PMH) for linear reversible synthesis (i.e., CNOT minimization with no topological constraints), and more recently by Reinforcement Learning (RL) based strategies in the more complex case of topology-aware synthesis, where each CNOT can act on a subset of all qubits pairs. In this work we introduce AlphaCNOT, a RL framework based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) that address effectively the CNOT minimization problem by modeling it as a planning problem. In contrast to other RL- based solution, our method is model-based, i.e. it can leverage lookahead search to evaluate future trajectories, thus finding more efficient sequences of CNOTs. Our method achieves a reduction of up to 32% in CNOT gate count compared to PMH baseline on linear reversible synthesis, while in the constraint version we report a consistent gate count reduction on a variety of topologies with up to 8 qubits, with respect to state-of-the-art RL-based solutions. Our results suggest the combination of RL with search-based strategies can be applied to different circuit optimization tasks, such as Clifford minimization, thus fostering the transition toward the "quantum utility" era.

</details>


### [133] [GeoAgentBench: A Dynamic Execution Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Agents in Spatial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13888)
*Bo Yu,Cheng Yang,Dongyang Hou,Chengfu Liu,Jiayao Liu,Chi Wang,Zhiming Zhang,Haifeng Li,Wentao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GeoAgentBench，一个用于评估LLM驱动的GIS智能体的动态交互式基准，包含117个GIS工具和53个空间分析任务，并设计了新的评估指标PEA和VLM验证方法；提出了Plan-and-React智能体架构，显著提升了多步推理和错误恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要依赖静态文本或代码匹配，忽视了动态运行时反馈和空间输出的多模态特性，难以有效评估复杂的、多步骤的地理空间工作流程，因此需要一个新的评估框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 构建GeoAgentBench基准，包含集成117个原子GIS工具的执行沙盒，覆盖6个核心GIS领域的53个典型空间分析任务；2. 设计参数执行准确性（PEA）指标，采用“最后尝试对齐”策略量化隐式参数推断的保真度；3. 提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的验证方法评估数据空间精度和制图风格遵循度；4. 开发Plan-and-React智能体架构，解耦全局编排与步骤级反应式执行以模仿专家认知工作流。

Result: 在七种代表性LLM上的大量实验表明，Plan-and-React范式显著优于传统框架，在逻辑严谨性与执行鲁棒性之间实现了最佳平衡，特别是在多步推理和错误恢复方面表现出色。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前LLM在自主地理空间分析中的能力边界，并为评估和推进下一代自主GeoAI建立了稳健的标准。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Geographic Information Systems (GIS) marks a paradigm shift toward autonomous spatial analysis. However, evaluating these LLM-based agents remains challenging due to the complex, multi-step nature of geospatial workflows. Existing benchmarks primarily rely on static text or code matching, neglecting dynamic runtime feedback and the multimodal nature of spatial outputs. To address this gap, we introduce GeoAgentBench (GABench), a dynamic and interactive evaluation benchmark tailored for tool-augmented GIS agents. GABench provides a realistic execution sandbox integrating 117 atomic GIS tools, encompassing 53 typical spatial analysis tasks across 6 core GIS domains. Recognizing that precise parameter configuration is the primary determinant of execution success in dynamic GIS environments, we designed the Parameter Execution Accuracy (PEA) metric, which utilizes a "Last-Attempt Alignment" strategy to quantify the fidelity of implicit parameter inference. Complementing this, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) based verification is proposed to assess data-spatial accuracy and cartographic style adherence. Furthermore, to address the frequent task failures caused by parameter misalignments and runtime anomalies, we developed a novel agent architecture, Plan-and-React, that mimics expert cognitive workflows by decoupling global orchestration from step-wise reactive execution. Extensive experiments with seven representative LLMs demonstrate that the Plan-and-React paradigm significantly outperforms traditional frameworks, achieving the optimal balance between logical rigor and execution robustness, particularly in multi-step reasoning and error recovery. Our findings highlight current capability boundaries and establish a robust standard for assessing and advancing the next generation of autonomous GeoAI.

</details>


### [134] [AI-Assisted Peer Review at Scale: The AAAI-26 AI Review Pilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13940)
*Joydeep Biswas,Sheila Schoepp,Gautham Vasan,Anthony Opipari,Arthur Zhang,Zichao Hu,Sebastian Joseph,Matthew Lease,Junyi Jessy Li,Peter Stone,Kiri L. Wagstaff,Matthew E. Taylor,Odest Chadwicke Jenkins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次大规模实地部署AI辅助同行评审，AAAI-26会议所有主轨道投稿均获得一个AI生成的评审意见。调查显示参与者认为AI评审有用且在技术准确性等关键维度上更偏好AI评审，系统在检测科学缺陷方面显著优于简单LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学同行评审面临投稿量激增的压力，难以维持评审质量、一致性和及时性。尽管AI技术进步促使社区考虑将其用于同行评审，但AI能否在真实会议规模下生成技术上合理的评审意见仍是未解决的关键问题。

Method: 在AAAI-26会议进行首次大规模AI辅助同行评审实地部署，每个主轨道投稿获得一个明确标识的AI评审。系统结合前沿模型、工具使用和安全防护的多阶段流程，在一天内为22,977篇完整评审论文生成评审意见。通过大规模调查作者和程序委员会成员评估接受度，并引入新基准测试系统性能。

Result: 系统在一天内成功为22,977篇论文生成评审。大规模调查显示参与者不仅认为AI评审有用，且在技术准确性和研究建议等关键维度上实际上更偏好AI评审而非人类评审。新基准测试表明，系统在检测多种科学缺陷方面显著优于简单LLM生成评审基线。

Conclusion: 最先进的AI方法已能在会议规模下为科学同行评审做出有意义的贡献，为下一代人机协同评估研究开辟了道路。

Abstract: Scientific peer review faces mounting strain as submission volumes surge, making it increasingly difficult to sustain review quality, consistency, and timeliness. Recent advances in AI have led the community to consider its use in peer review, yet a key unresolved question is whether AI can generate technically sound reviews at real-world conference scale. Here we report the first large-scale field deployment of AI-assisted peer review: every main-track submission at AAAI-26 received one clearly identified AI review from a state-of-the-art system. The system combined frontier models, tool use, and safeguards in a multi-stage process to generate reviews for all 22,977 full-review papers in less than a day. A large-scale survey of AAAI-26 authors and program committee members showed that participants not only found AI reviews useful, but actually preferred them to human reviews on key dimensions such as technical accuracy and research suggestions. We also introduce a novel benchmark and find that our system substantially outperforms a simple LLM-generated review baseline at detecting a variety of scientific weaknesses. Together, these results show that state-of-the-art AI methods can already make meaningful contributions to scientific peer review at conference scale, opening a path toward the next generation of synergistic human-AI teaming for evaluating research.

</details>


### [135] [[Emerging Ideas] Artificial Tripartite Intelligence: A Bio-Inspired, Sensor-First Architecture for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13959)
*You Rim Choi,Subeom Park,Hyung-Sin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ATI的生物启发式、传感器优先的三层架构，用于解决物理AI在延迟、能耗等方面的约束问题，实现了传感器控制与推理的协同设计，在移动相机原型上显著提升了准确率并减少了云端调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从数据中心向机器人和可穿戴设备转移，单纯扩大模型规模已不足以应对物理AI面临的严格延迟、能耗、隐私和可靠性约束。物理AI的性能不仅取决于模型容量，还依赖于在动态环境中通过可控传感器获取信号的方式。

Method: 提出了人工三元智能（ATI）架构，该架构在系统层面分为三层：脑干层（L1）提供反射性安全和信号完整性控制，小脑层（L2）执行连续传感器校准，大脑推理子系统（L3/L4）支持常规技能选择与执行、协调以及深度推理。这种模块化组织允许传感器控制、自适应感知、边缘-云执行和基础模型推理在一个闭环架构中协同演化，同时在设备上保留时间关键的感觉和控制，仅在需要时调用高级推理。

Result: 在动态光照和运动条件下的移动相机原型中实现和验证了ATI架构。与默认自动曝光设置相比，采用ATI的L1/L2自适应感知在路由评估（L3-L4分割推理）中将端到端准确率从53.8%提升至88%，同时减少了43.3%的远程L4调用。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了在具身AI系统中协同设计感知和推理的价值，传感器优先的架构方法能够有效平衡性能与资源约束，为物理AI系统设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: As AI moves from data centers to robots and wearables, scaling ever-larger models becomes insufficient. Physical AI operates under tight latency, energy, privacy, and reliability constraints, and its performance depends not only on model capacity but also on how signals are acquired through controllable sensors in dynamic environments. We present Artificial Tripartite Intelligence (ATI), a bio-inspired, sensor-first architectural contract for physical AI. ATI is tripartite at the systems level: a Brainstem (L1) provides reflexive safety and signal-integrity control, a Cerebellum (L2) performs continuous sensor calibration, and a Cerebral Inference Subsystem spanning L3/L4 supports routine skill selection and execution, coordination, and deep reasoning. This modular organization allows sensor control, adaptive sensing, edge-cloud execution, and foundation model reasoning to co-evolve within one closed-loop architecture, while keeping time-critical sensing and control on device and invoking higher-level inference only when needed. We instantiate ATI in a mobile camera prototype under dynamic lighting and motion. In our routed evaluation (L3-L4 split inference), compared to the default auto-exposure setting, ATI (L1/L2 adaptive sensing) improves end-to-end accuracy from 53.8% to 88% while reducing remote L4 invocations by 43.3%. These results show the value of co-designing sensing and inference for embodied AI.

</details>


### [136] [Reward Design for Physical Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13993)
*Derek Lilienthal,Manisha Mukherjee,Sameera Horawalavithana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了GRPO训练VLM时奖励设计对物理推理的影响，发现准确率奖励效果最佳，不同奖励诱导特定领域推理行为，注意力权重奖励显著提升空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在物理推理任务上远未达到人类水平，虽然SFT和GRPO等后训练算法在语言模型中表现出色，但奖励设计如何影响VLM的物理推理行为仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 在PhyX基准（包含6个物理领域和6种推理类型的3000个问题）上，使用IBM Granite Vision 3.3（2B）模型，系统性比较四种GRPO奖励信号：格式合规性、答案准确率、组合评分奖励（答案正确性+物理原理识别+单位一致性）、以及基于模型注意力权重的内部奖励。

Result: 基于准确率的奖励在大多数领域优于SFT，提供最强的整体提升；评分奖励改善结构化推理质量但无一致的准确率提升；注意力奖励增强空间推理（准确率从0.27提升至0.50），但损害符号领域性能。奖励设计产生领域特定的推理行为，而非均匀提升性能。

Conclusion: 奖励设计对VLM物理推理具有关键影响，不同奖励类型诱导不同推理行为。基于注意力权重的内部奖励无需空间标注即可有效监督模型的视觉关注区域，为视觉基础物理推理提供了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Physical reasoning over visual inputs demands tight integration of visual perception, domain knowledge, and multi-step symbolic inference. Yet even state-of-the-art Vision Language Models (VLMs) fall far short of human performance on physics benchmarks. While post-training algorithms such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) have demonstrated strong reasoning gains in language models, how reward design shapes VLM physical reasoning behavior remains poorly understood. We present a systematic reward ablation study for GRPO-based VLM training on physical reasoning. We compare four reward signals of increasing semantic richness: format compliance, answer accuracy, a composite rubric reward (answer correctness, physics principle identification, and unit consistency), and a novel internal reward derived from model attention weights over input image regions. We evaluate on PhyX, a 3,000-problem benchmark spanning six physics domains and six reasoning types across multiple-choice and open-ended formats, using IBM Granite Vision 3.3 (2B). Across both formats, GRPO with accuracy-based rewards outperforms SFT on most domains, though gains vary substantially by reward type and domain. Reward design does not uniformly improve performance. Instead, it induces domain-specific reasoning behaviors. Accuracy-based rewards provide the strongest overall gains. Rubric rewards improve structured reasoning quality without consistent accuracy improvements. Attention-based rewards enhance spatial reasoning while degrading performance in symbolic domains. Our internal attention-weight reward requires no spatial annotations and improves spatial relation accuracy from 0.27 to 0.50, suggesting that supervising where the model attends during generation is a promising direction for visually grounded physical reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [Memory Transfer Learning: How Memories are Transferred Across Domains in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14004)
*Kangsan Kim,Minki Kang,Taeil Kim,Yanlai Yang,Mengye Ren,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆迁移学习（MTL），通过跨领域的统一记忆池提升编码智能体性能，发现高层抽象洞察比低层轨迹更具可迁移性，平均性能提升3.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的自进化编码智能体方法通常将记忆利用限制在同构任务域内，未能充分利用跨领域编码问题共享的基础设施（如运行时环境和编程语言）。

Method: 提出记忆迁移学习（MTL），利用来自异构领域的统一记忆池；在6个编码基准上评估四种记忆表示（从具体轨迹到抽象洞察）的性能表现。

Result: 跨领域记忆使平均性能提升3.7%，主要通过迁移元知识（如验证例程）而非任务特定代码；高层洞察泛化良好，低层轨迹因过度特异化常导致负迁移；迁移效果随记忆池规模增长，且记忆可在不同模型间迁移。

Conclusion: 本研究为扩展记忆利用超越单领域孤岛建立了实证设计原则，证明记忆迁移学习的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Memory-based self-evolution has emerged as a promising paradigm for coding agents. However, existing approaches typically restrict memory utilization to homogeneous task domains, failing to leverage the shared infrastructural foundations, such as runtime environments and programming languages, that exist across diverse real-world coding problems. To address this limitation, we investigate \textbf{Memory Transfer Learning} (MTL) by harnessing a unified memory pool from heterogeneous domains. We evaluate performance across 6 coding benchmarks using four memory representations, ranging from concrete traces to abstract insights. Our experiments demonstrate that cross-domain memory improves average performance by 3.7\%, primarily by transferring meta-knowledge, such as validation routines, rather than task-specific code. Importantly, we find that abstraction dictates transferability; high-level insights generalize well, whereas low-level traces often induce negative transfer due to excessive specificity. Furthermore, we show that transfer effectiveness scales with the size of the memory pool, and memory can be transferred even between different models. Our work establishes empirical design principles for expanding memory utilization beyond single-domain silos. Project page: https://memorytransfer.github.io/

</details>


### [138] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Runtime Safety Shielding for Power Grid Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14032)
*Gitesh Malik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全约束的分层控制框架，用于电力网格操作，通过高层强化学习策略与运行时安全屏蔽相结合，在保证安全的同时实现了对未见过的网格拓扑的零样本泛化，为现实能源系统中的学习型控制器部署提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在电力网格操作任务（如拓扑控制和拥塞管理）中显示出潜力，但在实际电力系统中的部署受到严格安全要求、罕见扰动下的脆弱性以及对未见网格拓扑泛化能力差的限制。在安全关键基础设施中，不能容忍灾难性故障，基于学习的控制器必须在硬物理约束内运行。

Method: 提出了一种安全约束的分层控制框架，将长期决策与实时可行性执行显式解耦。高层强化学习策略提出抽象控制动作，而确定性运行时安全屏蔽使用快速前向模拟过滤不安全动作。安全性作为运行时不变量强制执行，独立于策略质量或训练分布。

Result: 在Grid2Op基准套件上进行评估，包括名义条件、强制线路停电压力测试以及在ICAPS 2021大规模输电网上的零样本部署而无需重新训练。结果表明，扁平强化学习策略在压力下表现脆弱，而纯安全方法过于保守。相比之下，所提出的分层安全感知方法实现了更长的情节存活时间、更低的峰值线路负载，以及对未见电网的稳健零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 安全性和泛化能力最好通过架构设计而非日益复杂的奖励工程来实现，为现实世界能源系统中可部署的基于学习的控制器提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has shown promise for automating power-grid operation tasks such as topology control and congestion management. However, its deployment in real-world power systems remains limited by strict safety requirements, brittleness under rare disturbances, and poor generalization to unseen grid topologies. In safety-critical infrastructure, catastrophic failures cannot be tolerated, and learning-based controllers must operate within hard physical constraints.
  This paper proposes a safety-constrained hierarchical control framework for power-grid operation that explicitly decouples long-horizon decision-making from real-time feasibility enforcement. A high-level reinforcement learning policy proposes abstract control actions, while a deterministic runtime safety shield filters unsafe actions using fast forward simulation. Safety is enforced as a runtime invariant, independent of policy quality or training distribution.
  The proposed framework is evaluated on the Grid2Op benchmark suite under nominal conditions, forced line-outage stress tests, and zero-shot deployment on the ICAPS 2021 large-scale transmission grid without retraining. Results show that flat reinforcement learning policies are brittle under stress, while safety-only methods are overly conservative. In contrast, the proposed hierarchical and safety-aware approach achieves longer episode survival, lower peak line loading, and robust zero-shot generalization to unseen grids.
  These results indicate that safety and generalization in power-grid control are best achieved through architectural design rather than increasingly complex reward engineering, providing a practical path toward deployable learning-based controllers for real-world energy systems.

</details>


### [139] [TREX: Automating LLM Fine-tuning via Agent-Driven Tree-based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14116)
*Zerun Ma,Guoqiang Wang,Xinchen Xie,Yicheng Chen,He Du,Bowen Li,Yanan Sun,Wenran Liu,Kai Chen,Yining Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TREX，一个能够自动化完成大语言模型（LLM）全生命周期训练的多智能体系统，并构建了FT-Bench基准进行评估，证明了其在优化模型性能方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型已使AI研究智能体能够执行单一的科学任务，但自动化复杂的现实世界工作流程（如LLM训练）仍是一个重大挑战。现有的方法难以整合从需求分析、文献调研、策略制定到模型训练与评估的完整流程。

Method: 提出了TREX多智能体系统，通过协调Researcher和Executor两个核心模块实现LLM训练全流程自动化。系统将多轮实验过程建模为搜索树，以高效规划探索路径、复用历史结果并从迭代试验中提炼高层见解。同时构建了FT-Bench基准，包含10个源自真实场景的任务，用于评估自动化LLM训练的能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，TREX智能体能够持续优化模型在目标任务上的性能，成功实现了从基础模型能力优化到特定领域任务性能增强的自动化处理。

Conclusion: TREX系统证明了多智能体协作在自动化复杂AI研究工作流方面的潜力，成功解决了LLM训练全生命周期的自动化挑战，为AI研究自动化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have empowered AI research agents to perform isolated scientific tasks, automating complex, real-world workflows, such as LLM training, remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce TREX, a multi-agent system that automates the entire LLM training life-cycle. By orchestrating collaboration between two core modules-the Researcher and the Executor-the system seamlessly performs requirement analysis, open-domain literature and data research, formulation of training strategies, preparation of data recipes, and model training and evaluation. The multi-round experimental process is modeled as a search tree, enabling the system to efficiently plan exploration paths, reuse historical results, and distill high-level insights from iterative trials. To evaluate the capability of automated LLM training, we construct FT-Bench, a benchmark comprising 10 tasks derived from real-world scenarios, ranging from optimizing fundamental model capabilities to enhancing performance on domain-specific tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the TREX agent consistently optimizes model performance on target tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Tensor Memory Engine: On-the-fly Data Reorganization for Ideal Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13319)
*Denis Hoornaert,Cole Strickler,Manos Athanassoulis,Marco Caccamo,Heechul Yun,Renato Mancuso*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件/软件协同设计的张量内存引擎（TME），通过在CPU数据路径中插入专用引擎来重构内存布局，为数据密集型应用提供理想的缓存局部性，从而缓解内存墙问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据处理从云端转向边缘，数据密集型应用面临内存墙挑战。大多数应用要么具有较差的局部性需要昂贵的重构，要么通过增加内存占用来换取局部性，现有系统难以满足性能期望。

Method: 采用硬件/软件协同设计方法，在商用SoC/FPGA平台上实现张量内存引擎（TME）。TME插入CPU数据路径中，通过代表CPU访问内存并重组内存布局视图来提供理想的缓存局部性，实现计算与内存访问的解耦。

Result: 提出了可在商用SoC/FPGA平台上实现的张量内存引擎方案，能够为运行的应用程序提供理想缓存局部性的数据访问，成功将计算任务保留在CPU上，而将数据重组任务委托给专用引擎。

Conclusion: 该方案通过清晰的计算与内存访问解耦机制，区别于存内计算和近存计算方法，为解决数据密集型应用的内存墙问题提供了新的技术路径，可有效提升边缘智能计算系统的性能。

Abstract: The shift to data-intensive processing from the cloud to the edge has introduced new challenges and expectations for the next generation of intelligent computing systems. As the memory wall continues to grow, modern systems can only meet these performance expectations by displaying data access patterns that exhibit ideal layouts in memory and ideal spatiotemporal locality in caches. However, only a few data-intensive applications are characterized by ideal locality. Instead, most applications exhibit either (i) poor locality when naively implemented and must undergo costly redesigns and tuning or (ii) inflated memory footprint to offer proper locality.
  To address the aforementioned challenges, we propose a hardware/software co-designed approach that can be implemented on commercially available SoC/FPGA platforms. Our approach seamlessly inserts in the CPUs' data path a Tensor Memory Engine that provides data with an ideal cache locality to running applications by (i) accessing the memory on behalf of the CPUs and (ii) composing a re-organized view of the memory layout. Unlike in- and near-memory computing approaches, it sets itself apart by clearly decoupling computing and memory accesses; computation is still performed on CPUs while the data re-organization is delegated to the Tensor Memory Engine.

</details>


### [141] [ATLAAS: Automatic Tensor-Level Abstraction of Accelerator Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13523)
*Ruijie Gao,Haoran Jin,Jirong Yang,Nathaniel Bleier*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了ATLAAS，首个基于MLIR的端到端流程，能够将RTL提取的加速器语义自动提升至张量ISA规范，实现了从硬件设计到软件栈生成的自动化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量加速器设计大多缺乏完善的ISA文档和编译器后端，限制了评估范围；虽有研究能从张量级ISA规范自动生成软件栈，但编写这些规范仍是繁琐的专家驱动过程，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 提出ATLAAS流水线，基于MLIR框架，以前期架构级模型提取产生的比特级LLVM IR为输入，通过8-pass语义提升流程逐步恢复高层张量结构，包括MAC习语、饱和语义、多维缓冲组织及数据布局变换，最终输出可用于ACT生态系统自动生成软件栈的张量ISA规范。

Result: 在Gemmini脉动阵列加速器上，ATLAAS将处理单元的比特级MLIR压缩高达92.9%，控制器模块压缩24-34%；发现了手工参考规范中遗漏的硬件特征，并通过Z3 SMT等价性证明验证正确性；在TVM的VTA处理器上，无需加速器特定修改即可提升所有四个数据通路模块，生成的编译器后端性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ATLAAS成功实现了从RTL到张量ISA规范的自动化提升，显著降低了人工编写规范的工作量，验证了该方法的正确性与通用性，为加速器设计到软件栈生成提供了完整的自动化路径。

Abstract: Numerous tensor accelerator designs have been proposed, yet most lack well-documented ISAs and compiler backends, limiting evaluation to a handful of operators. Recent work has shown that given a tensor-level ISA specification, complete software stacks including compiler backends can be automatically generated--but writing such specifications remains a manual, expert-driven process.
  We present ATLAAS, the first end-to-end MLIR-based pipeline that lifts RTL-extracted accelerator semantics to tensor ISA specifications.
